varalāṟṟup pēraṟiñar
mā. irācamāṇikkaṉār










nilavaṉ patippakam
nūṟ kuṟippu
nūṟpeyar	: 	maṟinta nakaram, poruni
āciriyar	:	varalāṟṟup pēraṟiñar
		mā. irācamāṇikkaṉār
patippāḷar	:	muṉivar ka. tamiḻamutu
patippu	:	2014

eḻuttu	:	11 puḷḷi
pakkam	:	14+138 = 152

muṉṉuri

ic ciṟu nūl, paṇṭit tirāviṭa nakaraṅkaḷāṉa moheñco-tarō, harappā eṉpavaṟṟi akaḻntu ārāynta aṟiñar veḷiyiṭṭa karuttukkaḷit tuṇiyākak koṇṭu, nam tamiḻakattuc ciṟu māṇavarkku eḷitil viḷaṅkum poruṭṭut teḷḷiya eḷiya naṭiyil eḻutappaṭṭatu. ittakiya aḻinta paḻiya nakaraṅkaḷ pala nam tamiḻakattil uṇṭu. avi koṟki, kāyal, pukār, uṟiyūr, vañci, māmallapuram mutaliyaṉa. avaṟṟil māṇavarkku oru paṟṟu uṇṭākavum, tamiḻnāṭṭup paṇṭi varalāṟṟiṉ perumpakuti iṉṟaḷavum maṇṇil puti yuṇṭutāṉ kiṭakkiṉṟatu eṉpati avarkaḷ aṟiyavum namatu tirāviṭa nākarikam varalāṟṟup pukaḻpeṟṟa ekiptiya nākarikattiṉum viñciyatu eṉpatiyum aḵtu intiyā muḻuvatum paraviyirunta taṉi nākarikam eṉpatiyum nam ciṟār aṟintiṉpuṟavum in nūl utavalām. ittakiya ciṟu nūlkaḷ pala veḷivaral nāṭṭukku naṉmi viḷikkum.

cēkkiḻār akam,	
28-11-45								mā. irācamāṇikkam
patippuri

moḻiyālum, iṉattālum, aṟivālum ciṟantōṅki viḷaṅkiya paḻantamiḻkkulam paṭip paṭiyāy tāḻcciyuṟṟu mīḷa muṭiyāta aṭimic cakatiyilum, aṟiyāmip paḷḷattilum vīḻntu kiṭanta araciyal kumukāya varalāṟṟu uṇmi kaḷit tēṭi eṭuttu vaḻaṅkuvati nōkkamākak koṇṭu em tantiyār tamiḻmaṇ patippakattit tolinōkkup pārviyōṭu toṭaṅ kiṉār.
eṉ tantiyiṉ patippuc cuvaṭukaḷip piṉpaṟṟi em patippup paṇiyic ceytu varukiṟēṉ. tamiḻp pēraṟiñar muṉivar mā. irācamāṇikkaṉār ilakkiya āyvukaḷ, camayam cārnta āyvukaḷ, varalāṟṟāyvukaḷ, kōvil āyvukaḷ, kalveṭṭu āyvukaḷ, māṇavar nalaṉ kuṟittu avar  eḻutiya 110 nūlkaḷum āyvāḷarkaḷukkum māṇavar kaḷukkum, tamiḻ uṇarvāḷar kaḷukkum peritum payaṉpaṭattakka nūlkaḷākum.
ivaṟṟil 18 nūlkaḷi 2012il em patippakam veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu. toṭar paṇiyāka 2014il 21 nūlkaḷi tamiḻulakam payaṉpaṭum vakiyil em patippakam veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉi aṉivarum paṭittup payaṉpeṟa vēṇṭukiṟēṉ.

- ka. tamiḻamutu
nuḻiyumuṉ
maṉitaril taliyāya maṉitarē!

āciriyar, āyvāḷar, aṟiñar eṉṟu tam uḻippālum tiṟamiyālum viṭāmuyaṟciyālum paṭippaṭiyāka uyarnta irācamāṇikkaṉār tamiḻnāṭu kaṇṭa mikac ciṟanta cintaṉiyāḷarkaḷuḷ oruvar. moḻi, iṉam, nāṭu eṉat tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakam paṟṟi āḻac cintittavarkaḷuḷ avar kuṟippiṭattakkavar. camayañ cārnta mūṭa nampikkikaḷum, cātip piṇakkukaḷum, piṟamoḻi īṭupāṭum, peṇṇaṭimit taṉamum, caṭaṅku nāṭṭamum, kalviyaṟiviṉmiyum tamiḻc camutāyattic cūṟiyāṭik koṇṭirunta kāla kaṭṭattiltāṉ irācamāṇikkaṉār tam āciriya vāḻkkiyit toṭaṅkiṉār. tāmuṇṭu, tam kuṭumpamuṇṭu, tam vēliyuṇṭu eṉṟu avarāl irukka muṭiyavilli.
tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷip paḻutaṟap paṭittiruntamiyālum, inta nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟi aṭippaṭic cāṉṟukaḷiliruntu avarē akaḻntu uruvākki yiruntamiyālum mika eḷiya niliyiliruntu uḻippu, muyaṟci, ūkkam ivi koṇṭē uyarat toṭaṅkiyiruntamiyālum tammāl iyaṉṟatit tām vāḻum camutāyattiṟkuc ceyvatu tamatu kaṭamiyeṉa avar karuti yiruntār. moḻi nalam, tamiḻt tirumaṇam, cāti maṟuppu eṉpaṉa avaruṭiya toṭakkak kālak kaḷaṅkaḷāka amintaṉa. tāymoḻit tamiḻ, tamiḻariṭiyē peṟavēṇṭiya matippiyum payaṉpāṭṭiyum peṟāmaliruntami avari varuttiyatu.
`tamiḻ namatu tāymoḻi īṉṟa tāyip pōṟṟutal makkaḷatu kaṭami. atu pōlavē namatu piṟappu mutal iṟappu variyil namakku uṟutuṇiyāka iruntu nammi vāḻac ceyyum moḻiyik kāppatum vāḻvikkac ceyvatum tamiḻarākiya namatu kaṭami. `ï‹iwa jÄHuJ thœÉš jÄœ v›thW ïU¡»‹wJ? oru tamiḻaṉ maṟṟoru tamiḻaṉōṭu pēcumpōtu perumpālum piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷik kalantē pēcuvatik kāṇkiṟōm. ippiṟamoḻic coṟkaḷ nam moḻiyiṟ kalantu tamiḻ naṭiyik keṭuttuviṭukiṉṟaṉa. oru tamiḻc collukkup patilākap piṟa moḻic collip payaṉpaṭuttiṉāl, antat tamiḻccol nāḷaṭivil vaḻakku oḻintuviṭum `piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷik kalantu pēcuvatil taliciṟantavar tamiḻarē āvār. moḻik koli purivatil mutaṟparicu peṟattakkavar nam tamiḻarē āvar!
`nam tamiḻnāṭṭuc ceytit tāḷkaḷil tamiḻp pulamiyuṭiyār perumpālum illiyeṉṟē kūṟalām. ataṉālum, nalla tamiḻi vaḷarkka vēṇṭum eṉṟa ārvam iṉmiyālum, mikap palavākiya piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷik kalantu tamiḻ eḻuti varukiṟārkaḷ. ivaṟṟit `tamiḻc ceytittāḷkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkup patilāka `kalappu moḻic ceytittāḷkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟutalē poruntum. ivvāṟu ceytit tāḷkaḷil moḻik koli purivōr vēṟṟu nāṭṭavarallar, vēṟu moḻi pēcum ayalārallar. tamiḻakattil piṟantu tamiḻilēyē pēcivarum makkaḷāvar eṉpati veṭkattuṭaṉ kūṟa vēṇṭupavarāka irukkiṟōm.
nāṭu muḻuvatum moḻi nalam kuṉṟiyiruntamiyit tuṟi cārnta cāṉṟukaḷōṭum kavaliyōṭum cuṭṭik kāṭṭiyatōṭu irācamāṇikkaṉār niṉṟuviṭavilli. moḻiyi eppaṭi vaḷarppatu, kāppāṟṟuvatu, uyarttuvatu eṉpatē avaruṭiya toṭarnta cintaṉiyāka iruntatu. kālaṅ kālamāka aṭimippaṭṭuk kiṭanta camutāyam avar kaṇ muṉ niṉṟatu. vaṭamoḻi ātikkamum āṅkilappaṟṟum tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ kaṇkaḷi mūṭiyiruntaṉa. tam moḻiyiṉ, iṉattiṉ, nāṭṭiṉ perumi aṟiyātu irunta avarkaṭkut tamiḻiṉ toṉmiyiyum perumiyiyum ciṟappiyum eṭuttuc colvatu tam kaṭamiyeṉṟu karutiṉār irācamāṇikkaṉār. akkaṭamiyi niṟivēṟṟa avar kiyāṇṭa vaḻikaḷ pōṟṟattakkaṉa.
tammuṭiya māṇavarkaḷi avar mutaṟpaṭiyākak koṇṭār. nalla tamiḻil pēcavum eḻutavum avarkaḷukkup payiṟṟuvittār. ciṟuciṟu kaṭṭurikaḷi uruvākkap payiṟciyaḷittār. moḻinaṭi paṟṟi avarkaḷukkup puriyumāṟu kalanturiyāṭiṉār. moḻi naṭiyic cemmiyākkuvatu ilakkaṇamum pala nūlkaḷip paṭikkum payiṟciyumē eṉpati viḷaṅka vittār. ilakkaṇap pāṭaṅkaḷip paḷḷip piḷḷikaḷ virumpip paṭikkumāṟu eḷimippaṭuttiṉār. ataṟkeṉavē nūlkaḷi uruvākkiṉār. avariṭam payiṉṟa māṇavarkaḷ avar ilakkaṇam collik koṭutta aḻakiyum, paṭippaṭiyāka ilakkaṇatti nēcikka vitta tiṟaṉiyum pala iṭaṅkaḷil pativu ceytuḷḷaṉar. payilum nēram tavirtta piṟa nēraṅkaḷilum māṇavarkaḷuṭaṉ uriyāṭit tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ vaḷami kuṟittu avarkaḷic cintikkac ceytār. avariṭam payiṉṟavarkaḷuḷ palar piṉṉāḷil ciṟanta tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷākavum, nūlāciriyarkaḷākavum uruvāṉamikku ittaku payiṟcikaḷ uramiṭṭaṉa.
paḷḷi āciriyarāka irunta kālattilēyē otta ārvam uṭiyavarkaḷic cērttuk koṇṭu appakutiyilirunta potu makkaḷukkut tamiḻk kalviyūṭṭum paṇiyi avar ceytuḷḷār. `vaṇṇiyampatiyil taṉalaṭcumi toṭakkap paḷḷiyil pērāciriyariṉ tamiḻttoṇṭu toṭaṅkiyatu. aṅkut toṭarntu vakuppukaḷ naṭattiṉār. paṇikaḷil iruntavarkaḷukku vāra iṟuti nāṭkaḷil tamiḻ vakuppeṭuttār. uṟaviṉarkaḷik kūṭa avar viṭṭu vikka villi. `kuṭiyaracu itaḻil coṟpa ūtiyattil paṇiyāṟṟik koṇṭirunta tam mittuṉar pu. celvarācaṉi `vittuvāṉ paṭikka vittu, ceṉṉi aptul akkīm uyarnilip paḷḷiyil talimit tamiḻāciriyarākap paṇipeṟac ceytār.
tamakkuk kiṭitta vāyppukaḷi ellām moḻic cintaṉikaḷi vitikkap payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭavar, `tamiḻar nalvāḻkkik kaḻakam, `nakkīrar kaḻakam, `māṇavar maṉṟam mutaliya potu nala amippukaḷōṭu tammi iṇittuk koṇṭār. 1946 il ceṉṉi nakkīrar kaḻakam eṉṟa amippiṉit toṭaṅkiya kālattup pērāciriyar avarkaḷiṉ aravaṇippum toṇṭum kaḻakattiṟkuk kiṭittuk kaḻakam vaḷarntu ciṟantatu. 1946 ām āṇṭil nakkīrar kaḻakam `tiruvaḷḷuvar eṉṟa tiṅkaḷ ēṭṭiṉi naṭattat toṭaṅkiyapōtu, pērāciriyar tam kaṭṭurikaḷi vaḻaṅkiyatōṭu allātu, tām naṭpup pūṇṭirunta tavattiru īrā pātiriyāriṉ kaṭṭuriyiyum peṟṟut tantu itaḻukkup perumi cērntār. aṭiyavaṉiṉ tamiḻ toṇṭiṟku ūkkamum, uḷḷattiṟku uramum, tuvaṇṭapōtu taṭṭi eḻuppi ūṭṭa urikaḷum aḷittuc ciṟappittavar pērāciriyar eṉṟu irācamāṇikkaṉāriṉ tamiḻt toṇṭi niṉivu kūrntuḷḷār nakkīrar kaḻaka amippāḷar ciṟuvi nacciṉārkkiṉiyaṉ.
kalvi vaḻi viḻippuṇarvil perunampikki koṇṭirun tamiyāl, `araciyalārum camūkat talivarkaḷum nāṭeṅkum kalvik kūṭaṅkaḷi ēṟpaṭutta vēṇṭum. kalvi kaṟkum vayatuṭiya entac ciṟuvaṉum ciṟumiyum kaṟkāmal iruttal kūṭātu eṉṟu muḻaṅkiya ipperumakaṉār, tām vāḻnta pakutiyil irunta attaṉi kuṭumpaṅkaḷiṉ piḷḷikaḷum paḷḷip paṭippuk koḷḷumāṟu ceytuḷḷār.
peṇkaḷ piṉtaṅkiya kālam atu. `aṭuppūtum peṇṇukaḷukkup paṭippetaṟku eṉṟu kēṭṭavarkaḷ mikkirunta kālam. antak kāla kaṭṭattiltāṉ pērāciriyar peṇ kalvikkākak kural koṭuttār. eṭṭām vakuppē paṭittirunta tam maṉivikkut tāmē āciriyarāka iruntu payiṟṟuvittu avari, `vittuvāṉ paṭṭam peṟac ceytār. `eṉ kaṇavar eṉakku āṅkilap pāṭamum tamiḻppāṭamum kaṟpittu vantār. pāṭam kaṟpikkum nērattil paḷḷi āciriyarākavē kāṇappaṭṭār. ovvoru peṇṇum taṉṉik kāttuk koḷḷum vakiyil kalvi kaṟṟup poruḷīṭṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatu eṉ kaṇavar karuttu. ataṉāl, eṉṉip peṇkaḷ paḷḷiyil tamiḻāciriyarāka amarttiṉār. māṇaviyarkku moḻiyuṇarvum nāṭṭuṇarvum varumāṟu pēcavēṇṭum eṉṟu avar aṭikkaṭi kūṟuvār eṉṟu `eṉ kaṇavar eṉṟa kaṭṭuriyil tirumati kaṇṇammāḷ irācamāṇikkaṉār kūṟiyuḷḷami iṅkuk karutattakkatu.
moḻi, iṉam, nāṭu ivaṟṟip paṟṟi aṟintiruntāl tāṉ avaṟṟi nēcikkavum avaṟṟiṟkut tuṇi niṟkavum muṭiyumeṉpatil avar teḷivāka iruntamiyāltāṉ, `kalviyil akkaṟi kāṭṭiṉār. avaruṭiya āciriyap paṇi ataṟkut tuṇiyāṉatu. tammiṭam payila vantavarkku moḻiyuṇarvūṭṭiṉār. `tamiḻakattil āṭci tamiḻilēyē iyaṅka vēṇṭum. ellāk kalvi niliyaṅkaḷilum āṅkilam oḻinta ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷiyum tamiḻil kaṟpittal vēṇṭum eṉpatu avar koḷkiyāka iruntatu. aṟiviyal maṉappāṉmiyi ūṭṭi vaḷarkkum muṟiyil aminta pāṭanūlkaḷiyē piḷḷikaḷ paṭikkumpaṭic ceytal vēṇṭum. ulaka nāṭukaḷōṭu tam nāṭṭi oppiṭṭup pārttuk kuṟikaḷi niṟivākkum maṉappāṅku vaḷarumpaṭiyāṉa muṟiyil kalvi aḷikkappaṭal vēṇṭum. kaṭavuḷ paṟṟum, nalloḻukkamum, camutāya vaḷarcciyil nāṭṭamum ūṭṭat takka kalviyi ēṟṟa tiṭṭaṅkoṇṭu naṭi muṟikkuk koṇṭu varutal vēṇṭum eṉṟu avar eḻutiyuḷḷār.
`pēccut tamiḻē eḻuttut tamiḻukku aṭippaṭi ātalāl, namatu pēccut tamiḻ perumpālum tūya tamiḻc coṟkaḷik koṇṭatāka iruttal vēṇṭum. appoḻutu tāṉ nām eḻutum tamiḻ nalla tamiḻ naṭiyil irukkamuṭiyum eṉpatu avar karuttāka iruntamiyāl, tammiṭam payiṉṟa māṇavarkaḷi avar nalla tamiḻil pēcumāṟu vaḻippaṭuttiṉār. ataṟkākavē tām paṇiyāṟṟiya paḷḷikaḷilum kallūrikaḷilum irunta māṇavar maṉṟaṅkaḷic ceyalūkkam peṟa vittār. tamiḻ maṉṟaṅkaḷ illāta kalvi niliyaṅkaḷ avaṟṟip peṟumāṟu ceytār. pēcciyum eḻuttiyum iḷiñarkaḷ vaḷappaṭuttik koḷḷa utavumāṟu `vaḻiyum vakiyum eṉṟoru ciṟu nūl paṭittaḷittār. eṇṇaṅkaḷi eppaṭi uruvākkik koḷvatu, anta eṇṇaṅkaḷi veḷippaṭutta ettaku coṟkaḷit tērntu koḷvatu, accoṟkaḷi iṇittut toṭarkaḷi eppaṭi amippatu, piṉ attoṭarkaḷik kēṭṭārp piṇikkum takiyaṉavāy eṅṅaṉam aḻaku paṭuttuvatu eṉpaṉa paṟṟi nāṉku talippukaḷil aminta innūl iḷiñar kaḷiṭiyē perum varavēṟpip peṟṟatuṭaṉ irācamāṇikkaṉāriṉ moḻi vaḻic cintaṉikaḷukkum ciṟanta cāṉṟāka amintatu.
tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ toṉmi, perumi ivaṟṟit tamiḻarkaḷ aṟiya vēṇṭum eṉpataṟkākavē `tamiḻ moḻic celvam, `tamiḻ iṉam, `tamiḻar vāḻvu, `eṉṟumuḷa teṉṟamiḻ, `putiya tamiḻakam eṉṉum avaruṭiya nūlkaḷ tamiḻ makkaḷukku avarkaḷ maṟantirunta moḻiyiṉ perumiyi, ciṟappi aṭiyāḷappaṭuttiṉa. `oru moḻi pēcum makkaḷ tam moḻiyiṉ paḻimikaḷiyum perumiyiyum vaḷarcciyiyum naṉku aṟintāṟṟāṉ, ammoḻiyiṉiṭattu ārvamum ataṉ vaḷarcciyil karuttum ammoḻi pēcum tammiṉattavar mītu paṟṟum koḷvar. iṅṅaṉam moḻiyuṇarcci koḷḷum makkaḷiṭiyē tāṉ nāṭṭuppaṟṟum iṉavuṇarcciyum ciṟantu tōṉṟum. ātaliṉ, ōriṉattavar iṉavoṟṟumiyōṭu nal vāḻvu vāḻa moḻinūlaṟivu uyirnāṭi pōṉṟa tākum. immoḻi nūlaṟivu taṟkāppukkākavum, tam vaḷarccikkākavum vēṇṭaṟpālatu eṉpatit tamiḻ makkaḷ aṟital nalamākum eṉṟa avar cintaṉikaḷ innūlkaḷ makkaḷiṭiyē vēr piṭikkac ceytaṉa.
tamiḻ makkaḷukku moḻip paṟṟiyum, moḻiyaṟiviyum ūṭṭiya atē kālakaṭṭattil, avarkaḷi nāṭṭuppaṟṟu uṭiyavarkaḷākavum māṟṟiṉār. tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ perumiyi, varalāṟṟi inta nāṭṭil piṟanta ovvoru kuṭimakaṉum naṉku aṟintirukka vēṇṭum eṉpataṟkākavē, `tamiḻaka āṭci, `tamiḻk kalikaḷ, `tamiḻar nākarikamum paṇpāṭum, `tamiḻaka varalāṟu eṉṉum nūlkaḷi eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭār. nāṭṭukkāka uḻitta aṟiñarkaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷic ciṟuciṟu nūlkaḷākki iḷiñarkaḷ avaṟṟip paṭittuyya vaḻivakuttār. iḷiñarkaḷ paṭittal, cintittal, teḷital eṉum mūṉṟu kōṭpāṭukaḷik kikkoṇṭāl uyaralām eṉpatu avar vaḻikāṭṭalāka iruntatu.
moḻi, iṉam, nāṭu eṉum mūṉṟiyum tamiḻarkkut toṭarntu niṉivūṭṭal eḻutuvār, pēcuvār kaṭamiyeṉṟu avar karutiyami yāl tamiḻ eḻuttāḷarkaḷ eṅṅaṉam amital vēṇṭumeṉpataṟkuc cila aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷi muṉvittār. `tāmāka eṇṇum āṟṟal uḷḷavarum uṇmiyāṉa tamiḻppaṟṟu uṭiyavarumē nalla eḻuttāḷar. tamiḻ eḻuttāḷar tamiḻ ilakkaṇa, ilakkiyaṅkaḷip paṭittavarāka iruppatu nallatu. tāḻntuḷḷa tamiḻc camutāyatti uyarttap payaṉpaṭum nūlkaḷi eḻutuvatiyē eḻuttāḷarkaḷ taṅkaḷ ciṟanta kaṭamiyākak karuta vēṇṭum. camutāyattil iṉṟuḷḷa tīṇṭāmi, peṇṇaṭimi, mūṭa nampikkikaḷ, kaṇmūṭit taṉamāṉa paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷ mutaliya piṟpōkkut taṉmikaḷi vaṉmiyākak kaṇṭikkum neñcuṟuti eḻuttāḷarkku irukkavēṇṭum attakiya eḻuttāḷarkaḷ, `tamiḻar eṉṟa aṭippaṭiyil oṉṟu kūṭutal vēṇṭum eṉṟu avar viḻintār.
ataṉālēyē maturiyil tiyākarācar kallūriyil paṇiyāṟṟiya kālattil maturi eḻuttāḷar maṉṟatti uruvākki atu ciṟanta muṟiyil iyaṅkumāṟu tuṇiyiruntār. immaṉṟattiṉ talivarāka iruntu maṉṟattiṉ mutal āṇṭu viḻāvil avar āṟṟiya uri tamiḻ eḻuttāḷar kaṭamip paṟṟiya avaruṭiya aṟi kūvalāka amintatu. `tamiḻakattil pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka āṭci moḻiyāka irunta namatu tamiḻ piṟkālattil taṉatu ariyaṇiyi iḻantatu; ippoḻutu vaḷarntu varukiṉṟatu. eḻuttāḷarkaḷ itaṉi maṉattil patiya vittal vēṇṭum ataṉ tūymiyiyum perumiyiyum toṭarntu pātukāppatē taṅkaḷ kaṭami eṉa uṇartal vēṇṭum.
`makkaḷ pēcuvatu pōlavē eḻutavēṇṭum atutāṉ uyir uḷḷa naṭi eṉṟu collip pāmara makkaḷ pēccu naṭiyiyē eḻuttāḷar palar eḻuti varukiṉṟaṉar. pāmara makkaḷatu naṭi potumakkaḷukku naṉku teriyum; atit terintu koḷḷa eḻuttāḷar nūlkaḷi vili koṭuttu vāṅka vēṇṭiya tēvi illi allavā? kocci moḻi pēcum makkaḷukku vāḻkkikkut tēviyāṉa paṭippiṉikaḷi ūṭṭuvatōṭu, iṉiya, eḷiya, centamiḻ naṭiyiyum aṟimukam ceytu vippatutāṉ eḻuttāḷaratu kaṭamiyāka iruttal vēṇṭum. eḻuttāḷar taṅkaḷ eḷiya, iṉiya centamiḻ naṭikku makkaḷi aḻittuc cella vēṇṭumē tavira, makkaḷuṭiya pēccu nilikkut taṅkaḷi iḻittuk koṇṭu pōvatu muṟiyaṉṟu. ciṟanta karuttukkaḷōṭu piḻiyaṟṟa eḷiya naṭiyiyum potumakkaḷukku ūṭṭuvatu eḻuttāḷar kaṭami eṉpati avarkaḷ maṟantu viṭalākātu. ituvē aṟaneṟippaṭṭa eḻuttāḷar kaṭami eṉpati nāṉ vaṟpuṟutta virumpukiṟēṉ.
cātikaḷ oḻintu caṭaṅkukaḷ aṟṟa camayam neṟippaṭat tamiḻar, `tamiḻ vāḻvu vāḻa vēṇṭumeṉpatil avar karuttāka iruntār. ataṉāl tāṉ, vāḻkkiyiṉ toṭakka niliyāṉa tirumaṇam tamiḻt tirumaṇamāka amiya vēṇṭumeṉa avar vaṟpuṟuttiṉār. itaṟkākavē avar veḷiyiṭṭa `tamiḻar tirumaṇa nūl, tamiḻp periyārkaḷiṉ orumitta varavēṟpiyum pārāṭṭiyum peṟṟatu. tamiḻ nāṭṭaḷavil ataṟku muṉpō allatu piṉpō, ēṉ itunāḷ variyilum kūṭa vēṟentat tamiḻ nūlum itupōl tamiḻkūṟum nallulakattiṉ orumitta aravaṇippip peṟṟatāka varalāṟu illi.
`ellōrum vēli ceytu piḻikkavēṇṭum. picci eṭuppavarē nāṭṭil irukkak kūṭātu `valiyavar meliyavari ātarittāl nāṭṭil amitiyum iṉpamum perukum eṉṟu kūṟum irācamāṇikkaṉār, `kalvi maṭṭumē oruvarip paṇpaṭuttuvatilli. oḻukkam vēṇṭum. ellōrum oḻukkattiṟku matippit taravēṇṭum. oḻukkam uyiriṉum ciṟantatu. oḻukkattōṭu uṟiyum kalvitāṉ maṉitaṉi uyarvikkum eṉṟu teḷivupaṭuttiyuḷḷār.
moḻi, iṉam, nāṭu, kalvi, camayam, makkaḷ nalam, kōyilkaḷ eṉap palavum karutip pārttut tamiḻ moḻi ciṟakka, tamiḻiṉam uyara, tamiḻnāṭu vaḷampeṟap payaṉuṟu cintaṉi vitikaḷit tam vāḻnāḷ anupava aṟuvaṭiyiṉ payaṉāy inta maṇṇil vititta irācamāṇikkaṉār, `uṇmi pēcutal, uḻittu vāḻutal, muyaṟciyuṭimi, aṟivi vaḷarttal, nērmiyāka naṭattal, piṟarkkut tīṅku ceyyāmi mutaliyaṉa nēriya vāḻkkikkuriya koḷkikaḷām eṉṟu tām kūṟiyataṟku ēṟpa vāḻnta nūṟṟāṇṭu maṉitar. maṟupiṟappu nērntāl, `mīṇṭum tamiḻakattē piṟakka vēṇṭum eṉṟu avāvik kaṭṭuritta tamiḻmaṇ paṟṟāḷar. `avari muḻumiyākap paṭampiṭittuk kāṭṭum nūṟpā vaṭivilāṉa oruvari collaṭṭumā? eṉak kēṭkum avaratu keḻutaki naṇpar valli pālacuppiramaṇiyam colkiṟār: `irācamāṇikkaṉār matiyāl vittakar; maṉattāl uttamar, `maṉitaril taliyāya maṉitarē eṉum appar perumāṉiṉ tiruppūvaṇappatikat toṭar ipperuntakiyik karuttil koṇṭē amintatu pōlum!
ṭākṭar irā. kalikkōvaṉ
uḷḷuri

8. 	porunikkari nākarikam	141
1. maṇṇuḷ maṟinta varalāṟu

malippāṅkāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil irunta mikap paḻiya maṉitaṉ malikkukikaḷil vāḻntuvantāṉ. āṟṟaṅkarikaḷil irunta mikap paḻiya maṉitaṉ kaḷimaṇṇik koṇṭu cuvarkaḷi amittāṉ; taḻikaḷiyum puṟkaḷiyum koṇṭu kūri amittāṉ; iṅṅaṉam amitta kuṭicikaḷil vāḻntu vantāṉ. kāṭukaḷil irunta maṉitaṉ maraṅkaḷilum mara aṭiyilum vacittu vantāṉ. kaṭaṟkari ōraṅkaḷil irunta maṉitaṉ, aṅkirunta maraṅkaḷiṉ aṭiyil ciṟu kuṭilkaḷi amittuk koṇṭu vāḻntāṉ. ivvāṟu ovvōr iṭattilum vāḻnta maṉitarkaḷ avvavviṭattil iyalpākak kiṭitta kāy kaṉikaḷit tiṉṟaṉar; vilaṅkukaḷi vēṭṭi yāṭiṉar; āṟu, kaṭalkaḷil uḷḷa mīṉkaḷip piṭittut tiṉṟaṉar.
makkaḷ aṟivu nāḷaṭivil vaḷarcci peṟutal iyalputāṉē! aṟivu vaḷara vaḷara makkaḷ payirt toḻil, necavu, pēcakkaṟṟal mutaliyavaṟṟil tērcci peṟṟaṉar; tamakkuḷ pala camūkaṅkaḷi amittukkoṇṭaṉar; tam eṇṇaṅ kaḷit terivikkap palavakic cittira eḻuttukkaḷik kiyāṇṭaṉar; avaṟṟik kaḷimaṇ pāttiraṅkaḷmītu poṟittu vantaṉar; kaṟkaḷik karuvikaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttat toṭaṅkiyapiṉ kaṟpāṟikaḷmītum cittira eḻuttukkaḷit tīṭṭalāyiṉar.
kaṟkaḷukkup piṟaku, paṇṭi makkaḷ cempu, veṇkalam, īyam, veḷḷi, poṉ, irumpu ivaṟṟik kaṇṭaṟintaṉar; avaṟṟik koṇṭu pāttiraṅkaḷ, karuvikaḷ mutaliyaṉa ceytu koṇṭaṉar; eṉiṉum, ip paṇṭi makkaḷ maṇ pāttiraṅkaḷiyē mikutiyākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.
im makkaḷuḷ oru nilattār oru camūkattār piṟitoru nilattāruṭaṉum camūkattāruṭaṉum nāḷaṭivil kalantu uṟavāṭiṉar; avarkaḷukkuḷ pōkkuvaravum vāṇikamum kittoḻilum tirumaṇak kalappum uṇṭāyiṉa. pala kuṭumpaṅkaḷ oṉṟākat takutivāynta iṭaṅkaḷil kuṭiyēṟi vāḻalāyiṉar. avviṭaṅkaḷ ciṟiyaṉavāyiṉ `ciṟṟūr eṉavum, periyaṉavāyiṉ `pērūr eṉavum, mikap periyaṉa āyiṉ `paṭṭiṉam eṉavum vaḻaṅkalāyiṉa.
aṟivum nākarikamum vaḷara vaḷara makkaḷ ulōkaṅkaḷi mikutiyākap payaṉpaṭuttat toṭaṅkiyatum - payaṉaṟṟa kaṟkaruvikaḷiyum maṭpāttiraṅkaḷiyum vilakki viṭṭaṉar. avi kavaṉippār aṟṟu nāḷaṭivil maṇṇuḷ putiyuṇṭaṉa. paṇṭi makkaḷ iṟantavari maṇ tāḻikaḷil putittu vantaṉar; aṭikkaṭi iṭam māṟi vantaṉar; ak kālaṅkaḷil payaṉaṟṟa paḻiya poruḷkaḷik kaḻittup pōtal vaḻakkam. aviyum nāḷaṭivil maṇṇiṟ putiyuṇṭaṉa. kaṭal ōraṅkaḷil irunta pala ūrkaḷ kaṭal kontaḷippāl tuṟakkappaṭṭaṉa. āṟṟōram amintirunta aḻakiya nakaraṅkaḷ āṟṟu veḷḷattiṟku añcit tuṟakkappaṭṭaṉa. pala nakaraṅkaḷ vēṟṟār varaviṉāl pāḻākkappaṭṭaṉa. cila nakaraṅkaḷ maṇ māriyāl aḻintaṉa. iṅṅaṉam pala nakaraṅkaḷ nāḷaṭivil tuṟakkappaṭṭaṉa; aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. avi pala āṇṭukaṭkup piṉṉar maṇ mūṭuṇṭu viṭṭaṉa; pala nakaraṅkaḷ putiyuṇṭa iṭaṅkaḷil aṭarnta kāṭukaḷ tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉa. pala nakaraṅkaḷ āṟukaḷiṉ aṭiyilum kaṭal aṭiyilum aṭakkamāyiṉa.
in nikaḻccikaḷ naṭantu āyirak kaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷ ākiviṭṭaṉa. piṉṉarp pala putiya nakaraṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa; avaṟṟuḷḷum pala mēṟ coṉṉavāṟē maṟintaṉa; cila iṉṟum irukkiṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉam pala nakaraṅkaḷ maṇṇuḷḷum āṟṟaṭiyilum kaṭalaṭiyilum maṟippuṇṭa ceyti ippoḻutu eppaṭi veḷiyākiṟatu? eṉpatu takunta kēḷvi aṉṟō?
avvakkāla makkaḷ eḻuti vitta cittira eḻuttukkaḷik koṇṭum cempu mutaliya takaṭukaḷil eḻutivitta paṭṭayaṅkaḷik koṇṭum kaṟ kampaṅkaḷil poṟittuḷḷa vācakaṅkaḷik koṇṭum paḻaṅkālap paṉi ōlic cuvaṭikaḷik koṇṭum ippaṇṭi makkaḷ vāḻkkiyi ārāycciyāḷar oruvāṟu uṇarntaṉar; `im makkaḷ vāḻnta iṭaṅkaḷ eṉṉa āyiṉa? eṉṟu, aiyantōṉṟum iṭaṅkaḷiṟ ceṉṟu maṇṇi akaḻntu ārāyntaṉar; maṇṇit tōṇṭutaṟku muṉṉarē pala iṭaṅkaḷil maṇmītē paḻaṅkālap pāṉi ōṭukaḷ, maṇikaḷ mutaliyaṉa citaṟikkiṭakkak kaṇṭaṉar; ūkkaṅkoṇṭu ap pakutikaḷi akaḻntu ārāyalāyiṉar; cila iṭaṅkaḷil paḻiya vīṭukaḷik kaṇṭaṉar; terukkaḷik kaṇṭaṉar; maṭpāṇṭaṅ kaḷik kaṇṭaṉar; palavaki ulōkap pāttiraṅkaḷiyum karuvikaḷiyum kaṇṭaṉar; maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷmītum ulōkac citivukaḷ mītum irunta cittira eḻuttukkaḷiyum, piṉṉar uṇṭāṉa eḻuttukkaḷiyum kaṇṭaṉar; avaṟṟi arumpāṭupaṭṭup paṭittu aṟintaṉar; paḻiya nūlkaḷil kūṟappeṟṟa ceytikaḷi ikkāṭcip poruḷkaḷōṭu oppiṭṭup paḻiya varalāṟṟi oruvāṟu aṟiyalāyiṉar.
ivvāṟu maṇṇittōṇṭi, maṟinta nakaraṅkaḷik kaṇṭaṟiyum muyaṟciyē putiporuḷ ārāycci eṉappaṭum. inta ārāycci ulakam muḻuvatilum naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu. ekiptu, irāk, cīṉam, intiyā ākiyaviyē paḻiya nākarikam koṇṭa nāṭukaḷ eṉṟu, iṉṟaḷavum naṭipeṟṟa ārāycciyāl ārāycciyāḷar muṭivu kaṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar; innāṭukaḷ ēṟakkuṟiya 5,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē uyarnta nākarikattil tikaḻntavi eṉṟum meyppittu irukkiṉṟaṉar. itu niṟka.
nīṅkaḷ paṭikkum intiya varalāṟṟu nūlil, `intiyāviṉ paḻaṅkuṭikaḷ muṇṭar, tirāviṭar, eṉpavarāvar. avarkaṭkup piṉ intiyā vantavar āriyar. avarkaḷ cintu natikkariyil irunta paḻaṅkuṭikaḷi viraṭṭit taṅkaḷit tāpittuk koṇṭaṉar eṉṟu paṭikkiṟīrkaḷ allavā? anta āriyar pāṭiyuḷḷa rik vētattilum piṟkālap purāṇaṅkaḷilum āriyarkkum āriyar allāta paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷukkum pala caṇṭikaḷ naṭantaṉa eṉpatip paṭikkiṟōm; appaṇṭi makkaḷ uyarnta nākarikattil vāḻntavarkaḷ eṉpatiyum vācikkiṟōm; āriyar camakkirutam pēciya makkaḷ. tirāviṭar iṉṟu tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, maliyāḷam mutaliya vaṟṟip pēcum makkaḷuṭiya muṉṉōr eṉpatiyum nām paṭikkiṟōm; eṉavē, nam muṉṉōriyē āriyar etirttaṉar eṉpati aṟikiṟōm. aṟiyavē, `āriyar varumpoḻutu nam muṉṉōr vāḻntirunta nakaraṅkaḷ evi? eṉṟu āvalōṭu kēṭka virumputal iyalputāṉē!
nam muṉṉōr, āriyar varumuṉ, muṇṭar mutaliyōruṭaṉ kalantu intiyā muḻuvatum iruntu vantaṉar. āriyar vantapoḻutu cintu yāṟṟaṅkariyilum cintu-pañcāp maṇṭilaṅkaḷilum nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa aḻakiya nakaraṅkaḷi amittukkoṇṭu vāḻntiruntaṉar; avi āriyar paṭiyeṭuppālum āṟṟu veḷḷattālum tuṟakkappaṭṭaṉa. avi iṉṟu maṇmēṭiṭṭuk kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ iraṇṭē ciṟanta muṟiyil tōṇṭap peṟṟavi. aviyē moheñcotarō, harappā eṉpavi. muṉṉatu cintu maṇṭilattil irukkiṟatu; piṉṉatu pañcāp maṇṭilattil irukkiṟatu. ivi 5,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭavi eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar aṟikiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟiṉ vivaram aṭutta pakutiyiṟ kāṇka.
2. nakara amippu

moheñco-tarō nakaram amintuḷḷa iṭattil pala maṇmēṭukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟiyē ārāycciyāḷar tōṇṭip pārttaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ periyatu 1300 kecam nīḷamum 670 kecam akalamum uṭiyatu. itaṟku aṭuttatu 440 kecam nīḷamum 330 kecam akalamum uṭiyatu. inta iraṇṭu maṇmēṭukaḷic cuṟṟilum ciṟu maṇmēṭukaḷ pala irukkiṉṟaṉa. muṉṉavi nakaramākavum piṉṉavi nakarattiṉ pakutikaḷākavum irukkalām eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar niṉikkiṉṟaṉar.
moheñco-tarō 5000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ cintu yāṟṟiṉ kari ōramē iruntatu. cintuyāṟu aṭikkaṭi taṉ pōkki māṟṟikkoḷvatuṇṭu. ataṉāl iṉṟu aḵtu ēṟakkuṟiya aintu mil tolivil irukkiṟatu. harappā nakaram rāvi āṟṟaṅ kariyil amintu iruntatu. paṭakukaḷiṉ pōkku varavu karutiyē innakaraṅkaḷ āṟṟōram amikkap paṭṭaṉa. innakaraṅkaḷil paṭakukaḷil ēṟṟappaṭum poruḷkaḷ taṭiyiṉṟic ceṉṟu arapik kaṭali aṭiyum; aṅkup periya paṭakukaḷil allatu kappalkaḷil ēṟṟap peṟṟuk kuṟippiṭṭa nāṭukaḷi aṭiyum. inta vāṇipa vacatikkākavē inta nakaraṅkaḷ akkālattil ciṟappuppeṟṟu iruntaṉa.
moheñco-tarō amintuḷḷa iṭam mikap paranta camaveḷi ākum. nakaram oru caturakkal parappuṭiyatu. atil pattil oru pākamē tōṇṭappeṟṟu, ārāycci naṭi peṟṟuḷḷatu. in nakaram aḻivuṟāta niliyil iruppatu pārāṭṭattakkatu. harappā nakaram aḻintupaṭṭa niliyil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ataṉ kaṭṭaṭap pakutikaḷik kaṇṭa makkaḷ ceṅkaṟkaḷi ellām eṭuttuc ceṉṟaṉar; pukivaṇṭip pāti amippavarum pala poruḷkaḷi eṭuttuk koṇṭaṉar. ivaṟṟāl, annakara amippu mutaliyavaṟṟi uḷḷavāṟu kaṇṭaṟiyak kūṭavilli.
moheñco-tarōviṉ terukkaḷ kāṟṟu vacatiyik karutik kiḻakku mēṟkākavum teṟku vaṭakkākavum amintuḷḷaṉa. periya terukkaḷic ciṟiya terukkaḷ nērāka veṭṭic celkiṉṟaṉa. periya terukkaḷ arikkal mutal mukkāl kal aḷavu nīḷam uṭiyaṉa; 20 mutal 33 aṭi akalam uṭiyaṉa. periya terukkaḷil mūṉṟu vaṇṭikaḷ orē kālattil pōkalām. 9 mutal 18 aṭi akalamuḷḷa terukkaḷ pala. nāṉkaṭi akalamuṭiya cantukaḷ pala. iccantukkaḷukkum naṭuttarat terukkaḷukkum periya terukkaḷukkum iṇippu irukkiṉṟatu; cila terukkaḷ ceṅkal citivukaḷiyum maṭpāṇṭac citivukaḷiyum koṇṭu keṭṭippaṭuttap peṟṟu irukkiṉṟaṉa. cila puḻuti paṭintu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. terukkōṭi vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷ, vaṇṭikaḷ uṟiyātapaṭi vaḷivāka amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvaḷavu muṉyōcaṉi yōṭu amikkap peṟṟa vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷik kaṇṭu, akkāla makkaḷiṉ aṟivut tiṟaṉi ārāycciyāḷar viyakkiṉṟaṉar.
ovvoru teruvilum oḻuṅkāṉa kālvāy (kaḻivunīrp pāti) iruttal viyappiṉum viyappē! vīṭṭiliruntu cellum ciṟiya kālvāy terukkālvāyuṭaṉ kalakkum iṭattil caturakkuḻi kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cila iṭaṅkaḷil mūṉṟaṭi uyaramuṭiya tāḻikaḷ putikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. attāḻikaḷiṉ aṭiyil. nīrip peruṅ kālvāyil pōkac ceytu kuppiyit tēkkattakka ciṟu tuḷikaḷ iruttali nōkka, cintuveḷi makkaḷatu nuṇṇaṟivu viyattaṟku uriyatē aṉṟō! mēl māṭaṅkaḷiliruntu kaḻinīrk kuḻikaḷ cuvarukku uḷḷēyē amikkappaṭṭu iruttal piṉṉum viyappi viḷikkiṟatu. terukkaḷil ōṭum periya kālvāykaḷ mītu caturak kaṟkaḷ mūṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; cila iṭaṅkaḷil ceṅkal-karuṅkal-mūṭikaḷum iṭappeṟṟu uḷḷaṉa. nīṇṭa kālvāykaḷukku iṭiyiṭiyē mūli muṭukkukaḷil periya toṭṭikaḷ putikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiliruntu kaḻivunīr vēṟu kālvāykaḷ mūlam pirintu nakarukku veḷiyē celuttap paṭṭatu. periya kālvāykaḷ muṭiyum iṭaṅkaḷil aintaṭi uyaramum iraṇṭari aṭi akalamum uṭiya matakukaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi veḷḷa nīriyum vaṭikkat takkavi. ivaṟṟi ellām pārtta ārāycciyāḷar itu pōṉṟa cukātāra amippu appaṇṭikkālattu irunta vēṟu nakaraṅkaḷil illi eṉṟu kūṟi viyakkiṉṟaṉar. itaṉi nōkka, namatu nākarikam evvaḷavu ciṟappuṭiyatu eṉpati eṇṇip pāruṅkaḷ.
ovvōr illattilum malam kaḻikkum otukkiṭam irukkiṟatu. atu naṉṟākat taḷavarici iṭappeṟṟut tūymiyāka irukkiṟatu. atu nāṭōṟum nakara capit tōṭṭikaḷāl tūymi ceyyappaṭṭu vantatu. mēl māṭaṅkaḷilum ittakiya otukkiṭaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa.
moheñco-tarō ciṟanta vāṇipa nakaramāka iruntatu. ataṉāl aṅku iruntavar nalla nākarikam koṇṭa makkaḷāvar. eṉavē, terukkaḷ kāṟṟu vacati uṭiyaṉavāka amikkappaṭṭaṉa; terukkaḷ ellām iṇippuṇṭu kiṭantaṉa; cukātāram ciṟanta muṟiyil kavaṉikkappaṭṭatu. vinōtap potu iṭaṅkaḷ pala iruntaṉa. kālvāy vacati naṉmuṟiyil amikkappeṟṟu iruntatu. periya terukkaḷiṉ kōṭiyil kāvaṟkūṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. ciṟṟuṇṭiccālikaḷ iruntaṉa. ivaṟṟi nōkka, ivi ellāvaṟṟiyum kaṇṇuṅ karuttumākak kavaṉikkattakka nakarāṇmik kaḻakam oṉṟu iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu teḷivu.
mikka pukaḻpaṭitta ekiptiyarum pāpilōṉiyarum cumēriyarum appaḻiya kālattil kaṇṭirāta nakara amippu muṟiyi ic cintu veḷi makkaḷ kiyāṇṭu iruntaṉar. ivarkaḷ nākarikamē ulakil uyarntatākum eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟi makiḻkiṉṟaṉar.
3. kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil uḷḷa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ ellām iruvakic ceṅkaṟkaḷāl ākiyavi: maḻi, veyil, kāṟṟu ivaṟṟāl tuṉpappaṭāta paṭi irukkac cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷē puṟaccuvarkaṭkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa; uṭ cuvarkaḷukku ularnta ceṅkaṟkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. kaḷimaṇ cāntu cuvarkaḷiṉ mēlpūccākap payaṉpaṭṭatu. cila vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷ taviṭu kalanta kaḷimaṇ cāntāl meḻukap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cuvar, tari, kūri immūṉṟum kaḷimaṇ cāntāl āṉaviyē. akkāla makkaḷ uttiraṅkaḷip pōṭṭu, avaṟṟiṉ mītu nāṇaṟpāy parappi, appāykaḷmītu kaḷimaṇ cāntik kaṉamāyp pūcik kūri amittaṉar. kūriyiyum tariyiyum cuvarkaḷiyum oḻuṅku paṭutta maṭṭap palaki payaṉpaṭṭatu.
ovvoru teruvilum vīṭukaḷ aṇi aṇiyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. akaṉṟa terukkaḷil uḷḷa vīṭukaḷiviṭak kuṟukiya terukkaḷil uḷḷa vīṭukaḷē naṉṉiliyil irukkiṉṟaṉa. ellāk kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ cuvarkaḷum paruttaviyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. avi mūṉṟari mutal āṟaṭivari taṭitta viyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. pala vīṭukaḷ iraṇṭu aṭukkuḷi uṭiyavi; cila mūṉṟu aṭukkukaḷi uṭiyavi; pala akaṉṟa muṟṟaṅkaḷi uṭiya orē aṭukkiṉi uṭiyavi. ellā vīṭukaḷum taḷam uṭiyavi. cila kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ kaṭikaḷāka iruntavi; cila kōvilkaḷ eṉṟu karutattakkavi; pala eḷiyavar vīṭukaḷ. vīṭṭu aṟikaḷ nalla kāṟṟōṭṭam pukattakkavāṟu caṉṉalkaḷ koṇṭavi. periya vīṭukaḷil kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ tariyil patikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. cila vīṭukaḷil camiyal pāttiraṅkaḷ vittaṟkuriya marappeṭṭikaḷ cuvarkaḷilēyē iṇikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
celvaruṭiya māḷikikaḷ akaṉṟu nīṇṭa tāḻvāraṅkaḷ, muṟṟaṅkaḷ, ciṟiya-periya aṟikaḷ ivaṟṟi uṭiyaṉa. mēl māṭaṅkaḷilum pala aṟikaḷ uṇṭu. mēl māṭattiṟkuc cellum paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ iṉṟum naṉṉiliyil irukkiṉṟaṉa. cila māḷikikaḷil mara ēṇi vikkap paṭṭiruntatu. mēl māṭaṅkaḷil nīrāṭum aṟiyum iruttal viyappiṟkuriyatē yaṉṟō!
ikkālattil iruttal pōlavē periya māḷikiyil peṇkaḷ irukkat taṉi aṟiyum, palar kalantu pēcat taṉi aṟiyum, paṭukki aṟiyum, cāmāṉ aṟiyum, camiyal aṟiyum iruttal pārāṭṭa ttakkatu. camiyal aṟikaḷ veḷiccamum kāṟṟōṭṭamum poruntiyavi. avvaṟikaḷil taṇṇīrvikkat tāḻikaḷ tariyil putikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; camiyal poruḷkaḷi vikka maṇcāṭikaḷ payaṉpaṭṭaṉa; kaḻivunīrik koṇṭu cella camiyal aṟi muṟṟattiliruntu ciṟiya kālvāy veḷic ceṉṟatu.
vīṭṭu vāyilkaḷ yāvum perumpālum aintaṭikku mēṟpaṭṭaṉavāka illi. periya vīṭṭu vāyiliṉ akalam ēṟakkuṟiya 8 aṭikaḷākum. avvīṭṭār erutu mutaliya kālnaṭikaḷi vaḷarttaṉar pōlum! avarkaḷ avaṟṟi muṟṟaṅkaḷilēyē kaṭṭivantaṉar. mēl māṭam 6 aṭikku mēṟpaṭṭa uyaram uṭiyatāka illi. vāyiliṉ uyarattiyum taḷattiṉ uyarattiyum nōkka, iṅku vāḻnta makkaḷ irākkil irunta cumēriyarip pōlak kuḷḷarāka iruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar aṟikiṉṟaṉar.
harappāvil peruṅ kaḷañciyam oṉṟu kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ nīḷam 168 aṭi; akalam 135 aṭi; 
uyaram 52 aṭi; cuvariṉ kaṉam 9 aṭi. iraṇṭu variciyāka iraṇṭu maṇṭapaṅkaḷ atil irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivi tāṉiyaṅkaḷ koṭṭivikkap payaṉpaṭṭavi. toḻilāḷar illaṅkaḷ ikkālattup pōlīcār vīṭukaḷ pōlak kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
5000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa kiṇaṟukaḷ pala iṉṟum nīrc curappi uṭiyaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. ārāycciyāḷar anta nīriyē parukiṉṟaṉar. ak kiṇaṟukaḷ ikkālak kiṇaṟukaḷip pōlavē amintuḷḷaṉa; kiṇaṟṟic cuṟṟilum taḷam cevvaṉē amintuḷḷatu; toṭṭikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa; ciṟu kālvāy irukkiṟatu. ellāk kiṇaṟukaḷum uruḷikaḷik koṇṭaviyē.
moheñco-tarōvil veḷippaṭṭa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷuḷ kuṟippiṭat takkavi araṇmaṉi pōṉṟa kaṭṭiṭamum. aḻakiya ceykuḷam oṉṟumē ākum. ceykuḷam ikkālap poṟiva lāḷarum (Engineers) kaṇṭu tikikkumāṟu amintuḷḷatu. taṇṇīr irukkattakka iṭattiṉ nīḷam 40 aṭi; akalam 25 aṭi; āḻam 8 aṭi. itaṟku oḻuṅkāṉa paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. kuḷam cuṟṟilum 4 1/2 aṭi naṭipāti uṭiya cuvar irukkiṟatu. ataṟkappāl kuḷattic cuṟṟilum 7 aṭi akalamuḷḷa peruñ cuvar oṉṟu amintuḷḷatu.
kuḷattiṉ aṭimaṭṭamum aṭimaṭṭac cuvarkaḷum ceṅkallum oruvaki nilakkīlum koṇṭu taḷa varici iṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. itil nīrip parappa mūṉṟu periya kiṇaṟukaḷ pakkattil irukkiṉṟaṉa. aḻukkāṉa kuḷattunīri appuṟap paṭuttak kuḷattiṉ mūli oṉṟil caturap puḻi oṉṟu irukkiṟatu. atu matakupōla amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, ic ceykuḷattiṉ vēlippāṭu oṉṟē ārāycciyāḷarip peruviyappukku uḷḷākkukiṟatu.*
ik kuḷattiṟku vaṭakkē eṭṭu aṟikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟukku mēlum aṟikaḷ iruntirukka vēṇṭum. avi camayac cārpuṭiya peru makkaḷ vāḻntaṉavāka irukkalām. kīḻ aṟikaḷil nīrāṭa vacati amikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. cuvarkaḷ taṭittavi; katavukaḷ uṟutiyāṉavi.
kaṭṭiṭaṅkaṭkup payaṉpaṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷ perum pālum orē aḷavuṭiyaṉa. aḵtāvatu, nīḷattiṟ pāti akalam; akalattiṟ pāti kaṉam. ivi tēvikku ēṟṟa vaṭivil ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila caturamākavum cila vaḷivākavum, cila mūlikaḷukku ēṟṟavāṟu mukkōṇamākavum amintuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟic cuṭṭa kāḷavāykaḷ pala vaṭṭavaṭiviṉavāka iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpataṟkuriya aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
4. vīṭṭup poruḷkaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil vāḻnta nam muṉṉōr taṅkaḷ vāḻviṟkut tēviyāṉa ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum peṟṟiruntaṉar. apporuḷkaḷ kaḷimaṇ, maram, cempu, veṇkalam, caṅku, veṇkal mutaliyavaṟṟāl ceyyappaṭṭavi. avaṟṟuḷ maṭpāṇṭap poruḷkaḷē pala. maṭpāṇṭap poruḷkaḷē piṟarāl koḷḷappaṭātavi. ātaliṉ aviyē uṇmi nākarikatti viḷakkuvaṉa. cintu āṟṟuk kaḷimaṇṇuṭaṉ aprakam, cuṇṇāmpu, teḷintamaṇ ivaṟṟik kalantu naṉṟāka arittu maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟic ceyyum vēṭkōvar palarāka moheñco-tarōvil iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷi merukiṭat takka karuvikaḷum akkālattil iruntaṉa.
maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ palavum cenniṟamum karuniṟamum koṇṭavi yākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. palavaṟṟiṉ mītu ūrvaṉa, paṟappaṉa, naṭappaṉa, nīntuvaṉa mutaliyaṉa ōviyaṅkaḷākat tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paṟavikaḷ, vilaṅkukaḷ ivaṟṟiṉ ōviyaṅkaḷē pala. vilaṅkukaḷiṉ ōviyaṅkaḷ pul tariyuṭaṉum kāṭukaḷuṭaṉum cērttu iyaṟkik kāṭciyāka eḻutap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. palavaki ilikaḷ, pūṅkottukkaḷ, ilikaḷ mītu uruvaṅkaḷ, vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ vaṭṭam, ivaṟṟik kuṟikkum ōviyaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila tāḻikaḷ mītu maraṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ palavakippaṭṭavi; pala vakik kiṇṇaṅkaḷ, ciṟu taṇṭiṉmītu poruttap peṟṟa taṭṭukaḷ, ciṟiya akalkaḷ, ciṟiya mūkkuṭiya ēṉaṅkaḷ, taṭṭukaḷ, tāmpāḷaṅkaḷ, vaṭṭilkaḷ, paṭikkaṅkaḷ, kuṭaṅkaḷ, tōṇṭikaḷ, cālkaḷ, ciṟitu kuṭivāṉa pāttiraṅkaḷ, malarvikkum kaṇṇāṭip pāttiram pōṉṟavi, pampara uruvil aminta cīcākkaḷ, ik kālac cīcākkaḷip pōṉṟavi, pāṉikaḷ, caṭṭikaḷ, nīrttoṭṭikaḷ, palavaki mūṭikaḷ, purimaṇikaḷ mutaliyaṉa. ivi allāmal pūcikkuriya maṭpāṇṭam, kaṉalcaṭṭi, kumiḻkaḷ koṇṭa tāḻi, oṟṟik kippiṭi allatu iraṭṭik kippiṭi koṇṭa pāttiraṅkaḷ, mik kūṭukaḷ, elip poṟikaḷ, palavaki viḷakkukaḷ mutaliyaṉa kuṟippiṭattakkavi. ivaṟṟiṉ vēlippāṭu kaṇṭu ārāycciyāḷar viyakkiṉṟaṉar.
cila tāḻikaḷiṉ veḷippuṟamum, ciṟiya pāṇṭaṅkaḷum, taṭṭukaḷum pīṅkāṉ pōlap paḷapaḷappākkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl, cintuveḷi makkaḷ appaṇṭikkālattilēyē pīṅkāṉ ceyyum muṟiyi oruvāṟu uṇarntiruntaṉar eṉpatu terikiṟatu. avarkaḷ ammi, kuḻavi, ural ivaṟṟip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. mā arikkum kal entiraṅkaḷ nirampak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. kal ūci, elumpu ūci, kaṟkōṭari, cempu arivāḷ, marak kaṭṭilkaḷ, nāṇaṟ pāykaḷ, kōrip pāykaḷ, mēcikaḷ, nāṟkāli pōṉṟa uyarnta marap pīṭaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa iruntaṉa.
viḷiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷ pala kiṭittuḷḷaṉa: maṇ koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭa vilaṅkukaḷ, paṟavikaḷ, ūrvaṉa, nīrvāḻvaṉa, āṇ peṇ patumikaḷ, ciṟu ceppukaḷ mutaliyaṉa nirampak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. kucci oṉṟiṉmītu paṟavi ēṟuvatum iṟaṅkuvatumāka iruttaṟkuriya vacatiyōṭukūṭiya karuvikaḷ cila kiṭittaṉa. cilukiluppikaḷ nērttiyāṉavi. mūṉṟu maṇpantukaḷ, vālpuṟam tuḷiyuṭiya kōḻi, kuruvipōṉṟa ūtukuḻalkaḷ kaṇṇik kavarvaṉavākum. `500 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ ceyyappaṭṭa ūtu kuḻalkaḷ iṉṟum ūtattakka niliyil irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟutal viyakkat takkataṉṟō? uṭal vēṟu talivēṟākac ceytu kayiṟṟāl iṇikkappaṭṭut tali acikkum erutukaḷ palavākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. kaḷimaṇ vaṇṭikaḷ cila kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. veṇkala vaṇṭikaḷ cilavē kiṭittaṉa. cokkaṭṭāṉ karuvikaḷ pala vaṭivaṅkaḷ koṇṭuḷḷaṉa.
cintuveḷi makkaḷ poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, veṇkalam, veḷḷīyam, kārīyam ivaṟṟik koṇṭu pala poruḷkaḷum aṇikaḷum ceytukoṇṭaṉar. poṉ, veḷḷi nakikaḷ cilavē kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avarkaḷ ivviraṇṭiyum kalantu `elakṭram eṉṉum putiya ulōkattiyum ceyyak kaṟṟiruntaṉarām; cempiyum veḷḷīyattiyum kalantu `veṇkalam ākkavum aṟintiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ cempiyum veṇkalattiyum urukki vārppaṭam ākkiṉar; takaṭukaḷākat taṭṭi āṇi koṇṭu poruttiṉar; ivviraṇṭu muṟikaḷāṟ ṟāṉ ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum ceytu koṇṭaṉar; poṉ, veḷḷip poruḷkaḷukkup poṭi vittup paṟṟavikkum muṟiyik kiyāṇṭaṉar.
katti, vāḷ, īṭṭi, ampu, uṭivāḷ, cutti, irampam, vēl, uḷi, vāycci mutaliyavi cempālum veṇkalat tālum āṉavi. palvēṟu aḷavuṭiya kōppikaḷ, nīrccāṭikaḷ, taṭṭukaḷ, tāḻikaḷ ivaṟṟiṉ mūṭikaḷ iṉṉa piṟavum cempālum veṇkalattālum iyaṉṟavi. tōl cīvum uḷikaḷum maḻittaṟ kattikaḷum kuṟippiṭattakkavi. maḻittaṟ kattikaḷ nāṉku vitamāka irukkiṉṟaṉa: (1) arivaṭṭa vaṭivil amintavi; (2) `ṭa pōṉṟu amintavi; (3) vaḷivuḷḷavi; (4) nīṇṭu nērāka muṉi maṭṭum vaḷivāka amintavi. ivi allāmal uḻu karuvikaḷ, necavāḷar karuvikaḷ, tuḷiyiṭum karuvikaḷ, koṭṭāppuḷikaḷ, iḻip puḷikaḷ mutaliyaṉavum akkāla makkaḷāl payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa.
cintuveḷi makkaḷ kaḻuttumāli, kāppu, mōtiram, kālkāppu, palavakik kaṟkaḷ patitta kaḻuttumālikaḷ, iraṇṭu mutal āṟu caraṅ koṇṭa mālikaḷ, iṭippaṭṭi (oṭṭiyāṇam), uḷḷē arakkiṭṭa kāppu, palavakippaṭṭa vaḷiyalkaḷ, ōviyam tīṭṭappaṭṭa vaḷiyalkaḷ, neṟṟic cuṭṭikaḷ, kātaṇikaḷ, mūkkaṇikaḷ, camayat toṭarpuḷḷa patakkaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi aṇintaṉar.
kaḻuttu mālikaḷ pārkka aḻakāṉavi. poṉṉālum paccik kaṟkaḷālum ceyta maṇikaḷ kōkkappaṭṭavi cila; kuḻavi vaṭivattilum uruṇṭi vaṭivattilum ceyyappaṭṭa taṅka maṇikaḷum nīlappaḷiṅkuk kallāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa maṇikaḷum cērkkappaṭṭa mālikaḷ cila; taṅkattāl taṭṭiyākac ceyyappaṭṭu iraṇṭu maṇikaḷic cērttup paṟṟavittuḷḷavi, niḷamākak kōkkappaṭṭa mālikaḷ cila; 27 maṇikaḷ pala vaṭiviṟ ceytu kōkkappaṭṭa mālikaḷ cila; 70 taṅkamaṇikaḷ koṇṭa mālikaḷ cila; 240 maṇikaḷ koṇṭa nāṉku caraṅkaḷ koṇṭa mālikaḷ cila. ivvāṟu palatiṟappaṭṭa kaḻuttu mālikaḷ kaṇkaḷik kavarukiṉṟaṉa.
maṇikaḷ naṉku merukiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; naṭuvil oḻuṅkākat tuḷiyiṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. tuḷiyiṭum karuvi mikavum nuṭpamāṉatāka iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. tuḷi vaḻavaḻappākavum oḻuṅkākavum iruppatik kāṇa, attoḻilāḷar ciṟanta payiṟci uṭiyavar eṉpatu teḷivākiṟatu. cila maṇikaḷil ōviyaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila paṭṭi veṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila taṅkak kavacam koṇṭavi. pala vaki nakikaḷik kāṇa, moheñco-tarōvil ap paḻaṅkālattil toḻil nuṭpam vāynta poṟ kollarum, maṇikaḷ ceypavarum, caṅku aṟuppavarum, piṟarum naṉmuṟiyil vāḻntaṉarātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu terikiṟatu.
pottāṉkaḷ palavakippaṭṭaviyākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟāl, ammakkaḷ caṭṭi aṇiyum vaḻakkam uṭiyavar eṉpati aṟiyalām. koṇṭi ūcikaḷ pala kiṭittaṉa. ivaṟṟāl ammakkaḷ kūntali muṭipōṭum muṟiyiyum piṉṉaliyum, aṟintavar eṉpati aṟiyalām. akkālap peṇṭir tali nāṭākkaḷiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar; cīppukaḷiyum kaṇṇāṭikaḷiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar; koṇṭiyip piṉpuṟamākavum talimītum pōṭṭuk koṇṭi ūcikaḷic cerukiṉar; kaṇkaḷukku mi iṭṭuk koṇṭaṉar; mukattiṟku oruvakip poṭi pūcikkoṇṭaṉar. curuṅkakkūṟiṉ, `immakaḷirip pōlat tūymiyilum aḻaki virumpiyatilum ciṟantavar piṟanāṭukaḷil akkālattil illi eṉṉalām eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar muṭipu.
5. uṇavu-uṭi-toḻil

cintu āṟṟuveḷi paruva maḻiyum āṟṟup pāyccalum ciṟappuppeṟṟu viḷaṅkiyatākum. ātaliṉ, aṅku irunta nilaṅkaḷ ceḻippuṟṟu iruntaṉa; vaṇṭal maṇ paṭintiruntaṉa; ciṟanta maruta nilaṅkaḷāka viḷaṅkiṉa. ātaliṉ, aṅkuk kōtumi, nel, vāṟkōtumi (pārli), parutti, paṭṭāṇi, eḷ, pērīntu, mulāmpaḻam mutaliyaṉa ērāḷamākap payirākkap paṭṭaṉa. iṉṉum appakutikaḷil nellum kōtumiyum ciṟappuṭiya viḷiporuḷāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. ip palavaki viḷiporuḷkaḷi appaṇṭi makkaḷ tāḻikaḷiṟ cēmittu vittaṉar.
cintuveḷi makkaḷ potuvākap pulāl uṇṇum vaḻakkattiṉar; cōṟṟilēyē iṟicciyik kalantu tayārikkum `pulavu eṉṉum oruvaki uṇavu avarkaḷāl amikkappaṭṭu vantatu. avarkaḷ cōṟṟil āṭu, māṭu, paṉṟi, āmi, mutali ivaṟṟiṉ iṟiccit tuṇṭaṅkaḷik kalantu `pulavu ceytaṉar; palavaki mīṉ iṉaṅkaḷi uṇṭuvantaṉar; pala tiṟappaṭṭa paṟavikaḷi uṇṭaṉar; piṟa nāṭukaḷiliruntu kappalkaḷ mūlam koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa ularnta mīṉ iṉaṅkaḷiyum iṟicci vakikaḷiyum uṇṭaṉar; iṟiccit tuṇṭaṅkaḷi ularttip pakkuvap paṭuttip periya tāḻikaḷil aṭittu avvappōtu payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar; ivaṟṟōṭu palavakik kāy kaṟikaḷiyum, pāl, veṇṇey, tayir, mōr, ney mutaliyavaṟṟiyum uṇavup poruḷkaḷākak koṇṭiruntaṉar; palavakip paḻaṅkaḷiyum uṇṭuvantaṉar.
avarkaḷ mā arittup palavaki uṇavup poruḷkaḷ ceytu vantaṉar; vāṇalcaṭṭi, āṭṭukkal, iṭṭali ūṟṟum caṭṭi ivi kiṭit tuḷḷamiyē immuṭipukkuc cāṉṟākum; macālip poruḷkaḷi iṭittu, avaṟṟip pala aṟip peṭṭiyil vittiruntaṉar; maram, cippi, caṅku, cempu, kaḷimaṇ, veṇkalam ivaṟṟāl āṉa palavakik karaṇṭikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.
cintuveḷiyil parutti ērāḷamākap payirāṉatu. ataṉāl am makkaḷ nūl nūṟṟu āṭikaḷi neytu uṭuttu vantaṉar. ēḻikaḷ cātāraṇap parutti āṭikaḷip payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar. celvar pū vēlippāṭu poruntiya parutti āṭikaḷip payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar; cilar kittāṉ pōṉṟa muraṭṭu āṭikaḷi uṭuttaṉar. cilar nārāl āṉa āṭikaḷiyum uṭutti iruntaṉar. avakḷ iṭakkiyum mārpum maṟiyumāṟu pōrvi pōrttuk koṇṭiruntaṉar; caṭṭikaḷ aṇintu vantaṉar; kaḻuttup paṭṭikaḷum payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar.
peṇkaḷ pāvāṭikaḷip peritum payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar; pāvāṭikaḷi iṟukka, maṇikaḷ cērttuc ceyyappaṭṭa oṭṭiyāṇam pōṉṟa arik kaccikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. ak kaccikaḷ cila muṉpuṟam mukappu vikkap peṟṟavi. paṇakkārap peṇkaḷ melliya mēlāṭikaḷi aṇintu vantaṉar. moheñco-tarō ciṟanta vāṇipa nakaram ātalāl, aṅkup pala nāṭṭu makkaḷum vacittiruntaṉar. avarkaḷuḷ maṅkōliyarum iruntaṉar. avarkaḷ taliyil oruvaki muṇṭācu iruntatu.
cintu veḷi makkaḷ mutaṉmiyākak koṇṭirunta toḻil vāṇipamē ākum. āyiṉum kottarkaḷ, kuyavarkaḷ, kaltaccar, marattaccar, kaṉṉār, poṟkollar, irattiṉa vēlikkārar, muttiri cetukkuvōr, caṅkaṟuppōr, mīṉ piṭippavar, vaṇṭi ōṭṭuvōr, nāvitar, tōṭṭikaḷ, kāvalāḷikaḷ, kappal toḻilāḷar, uḻavar, necavāḷar, tiyaṟkārar, piṉṉal vēli ceypavar, tanta vēlikkārar, maṇikaḷ ceypavar, pāy piṉṉuvōr eṉap palavakit toḻilāḷarum annakaril iruntaṉar. ivarkaḷ ellōrum kitērnta toḻilāḷar eṉpatu moheñco -tarōviṟ kiṭitta palavakip poruḷkaḷik koṇṭu naṉku aṟiyalām.
moheñco-tarōvil uḷḷa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷik kāṇak kottar vēlittiṟatti naṉku aṟiyalām. avarkaḷ kēvalam paṇattiṟkākat toḻil ceytavar allar; kalikkākavē ceytu vantaṉar. eṉpati naṉṟāka uṇaralām. viyappūṭṭum vitamāṉa viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷ, nuṇṇiya vēlippāṭu koṇṭa mikac ciṟiya poruḷkaḷ, elumpu kaḷiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum putittu aṭakkam ceyvataṟku aminta tāḻikaḷ, palavaki ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭap peṟṟa pāṇṭaṅkaḷ, kaḻinīrk kuḻikaḷ, olip perukkik kuḻikaḷ, ceṅkaṟkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟit tiṟampaṭac ceyta vēṭkōvar toḻil tiṟamiyi eṉṉeṉpatu!
oru naṭaṉap peṇ cili oṉṟu veṇkalattāl āṉatu; vārppaṭam koṇṭatu. ataṉ vēlippāṭṭik kaṇṭu ārāycciyāḷar viyakkiṉṟaṉar. melliya takaṭukaḷāka aminta īṭṭikaḷum ciṟiya - aḻakiya - kūriya paṟkaḷikkoṇṭa irampaṅkaḷum, kōṭarikaḷam, arivāḷ maṇikaḷum, iṭivāḷkaḷum, nīṇṭa vāḷkaḷum, ampukaḷum, piṟavum kaṉṉārtam toḻil tiṟamiyi viḷakkuvaṉa allavā? araṇmaṉikkuḷḷum kāḷavāykaḷi vittup pōrk karuvikaḷic ceytuvanta kaṉṉāra vēliyāṭkaḷ eppaṭip paṭṭa toḻil tiṟam vāyntavarāka iruttal vēṇṭum!
poṉ kāppukaḷ, vaḷiyalkaḷ, aṭṭikikaḷ, kaḻuttu mālikaḷ, avaṟṟil cērkkappaṭṭa poṉ maṇikaḷ, pū vēlippāṭukoṇṭa maṇikaḷ, pala vaṭivaṅkoṇṭa maṇikaḷ, nuṭpa vēlippāṭu koṇṭa talic cāmāṉkaḷ, cuṭṭikaḷ, kātaṇikaḷ, kaṭakaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa ceyta ap paḻaṅkālap poṟkollar evvaḷavu tiṟami vāyntavarāka iruntiruttal vēṇṭum.!
ikkālattil collappaṭum navarattiṉaṅkaḷ akkāla makkaḷ nakikaḷil patikkappeṟṟu iruntaṉa eṉiṉ, akkaṟkaḷic cōtittal, avaṟṟip paṭṭi tīṭṭutal, vēṇṭiya aḷaviṟkut takkapaṭi ceytal, merukiṭal mutaliya nuṭpamāṉa vēlikaḷil kitērnta irattiṉap paṇiyāḷar iruntiruttal vēṇṭum allavā?
ōr aṅkulattiṟkum uṭpaṭṭa aḷavuṭiya muttiri kaḷic ceytal, avaṟṟil palavaki uruvaṅkaḷic cetukkutal, akkāla vaḻakkil irunta cittira eḻuttukkaḷip poṟittal, nuṇṇiya irampam koṇṭu ōraṅkaḷi aṟuttal mutaliya toḻilkaḷik kāṇa, mohañco-tarōvil ciṟanta cetukku vēḷiyāḷar iruntaṉar  eṉpatu terikiṟataṉṟō?
caṅki irampattāl aṟuttu, aram cetukkum karuvi mutaliyavaṟṟik koṇṭu palcakkaram, caturattuṇṭu, mukkōṇat tuṇṭu, irutayam pōṉṟa vaṭivaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ic caṅkup poruḷkaḷ pala nakikaḷil patikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa eṉiṉ, ivi evvaḷavu nuṭpamākat tiṟampaṭa vēliceyyappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum.!
paṭakukaḷ, kappalkaḷ eṉpaṉa kaṭal vāṇipam ceypavarukkut tēviyāṉavi allavā? āyirakkaṇak kāṉa milkaḷ tūram carakkukaḷik koṇṭuc cellattakka kappalkaḷō, periya paṭakukaḷō naṉmuṟiyil ceyyappaṭal vēṇṭum allavā? ātalāl, cintu veḷiyil tiṟami peṟṟa kappal toḻilāḷar iruntiruttal vēṇṭum aṉṟō?
ellāt toḻilkaḷilum kaṭiṉamāṉatu maṇi ceyyum toḻilē mikac ciṟiya maṇikaḷik kaṟkaḷik koṇṭu ceytal, avaṟṟil orē aḷavuḷḷa vaḻa vaḻappāṉa tuḷi kaḷik kaṭital eṉpaṉa ciṟanta toḻil tiṟamiyik kāṭṭum cāṉṟukaḷām. nīḷa maṇikaḷ, taṭṭi maṇikaḷ, uruṇṭi maṇikaḷ, pala muṉiyuḷḷa maṇikaḷ, ōviyam koṇṭa maṇikaḷ eṉpaṉa kaṇkaḷiyum karuttiyum kavarvaṉavākum.
6. vāṇipam

cintuveḷi makkaḷ koṇṭirunta vāṇipam uḷnāṭṭu vāṇipam, veḷināṭṭu vāṇipam eṉa iraṇṭākak kūṟalām. mohañco-tarōviṟ payaṉpaṭuttiya cempu mutaliya kaṉipporuḷkaḷ irāca putaṉam, mattiya mākāṇam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭaṉa. māṉ kompukaḷ kāṣmīriliruntum, viramum veḷḷi kalanta īyamum āpkāṉittāṉatti liruntum, oruvaki uyartarap paccikkal vaṭaparmāviliruntum, tipēttiliruntum, poṉ, kōlār aṉantappūriliruntum koṇṭu varappaṭṭaṉa. oruvakip paccikkal micūril iruntum, uyartara `amēcāṉ eṉṉum paccikkal nīlakiriyilirunm, caṅku muttu mutaliyaṉa pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiliruntum koṇṭu cellappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟāl appaṇṭik kālattil intiya uḷnāṭṭu vāṇipam evvaḷavu ciṟappāka naṭantu vantatu eṉpati eḷitil aṟiyalām. iḵtaṉṟic cintuveḷi makkaḷ intiyā muḻuvatiyum naṉku aṟintiruntaṉar eṉpatum pulaṉākum.
cintuveḷiyiṟ kiṭitta eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷ, muttirikaḷ, ōviyaṅkaḷ, karuvikaḷ, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi naṉṟāka ārāynta nipuṇarkaḷ avaṟṟukkum ēlam, cumēr, mecopoṭṭēmiyā, ekiptu mutaliya nāṭukaḷil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷukkum uḷḷa toṭarpi aṟintuḷḷaṉar. ipporuḷkaḷiṉ orumippāṭṭālum, ēṟṟumati-iṟakkumatip poruḷkaḷālum nammuṉṉōr ceytu vanta veḷināṭṭu vāṇipatti oruvāṟu aṟiyalām.
(1) calavikkal cintu veḷiyil illi. āṉāl calavikkal poruḷkaḷ pala kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. calavikkal `ēlam eṉṉum nāṭṭil mikutiyākak kiṭikkiṟatu mēlum, calavikkal muttirikaḷil uḷḷa vēlippāṭukaḷ ēlam nāṭṭic cērntaṉa. ātaliṉ, avi annāṭṭiliruntē vantaṉavātal vēṇṭum.
(2) cumēriyā nāṭṭil cintu veḷiyiṟ ceyyappeṟṟa muttirikaḷ cila kiṭittaṉa.
(3) uṭaliṉ tuvāram uḷḷa muṭkaḷip pōṉṟa amippik koṇṭa maṇpāṇṭaṅkaḷ cila ēlam, cumēriyā nāṭukaḷil kiṭittaṉa. avi intiyāviṟkē uriyavi.
(4) oruvaki veḷḷip paccik kallāl ceyyappaṭṭa pāttiram oṉṟu pāyvaṭiva vēlippāṭu koṇṭatāka mohañco-tarōvil kiṭittatu. attakiya pāttiraṅkaḷ cumēriyā, ēlam, mecopoṭṭēmiyā nāṭukaḷil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.
(5) paccikkal pāttiram oṉṟaṉmītu nanti uruvam kōvilil iruppatākap poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itiliruntu appaṇṭik kālattilēyē nanti vaḻipāṭu cintuviliruntu cumēriyāvukkuc ceṉṟatu eṉpatu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
(6) muttirikaḷum piṟa pāttiraṅkaḷum ceytaṟkuriya uyartarak kaṟkaḷ cumēriyāvukku aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa.
(7) ekiptu, cumēriyā, ēlam mutaliya nāṭukaḷiliruntu muṟukkuṇṭa kompukaḷik koṇṭa cemmaṟiyāṭukaḷ cintu veḷikku aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa.
(8) cumēriyāviṟkuc cintu veḷiyiliruntu irattiṉa maṇikaḷ koṇṭu cellappaṭṭaṉa.
(9) cintuveḷi makkaḷ yāṉit tantam, yāṉit tantattālāṉa cīppukaḷ, caturaṅkaviḷiyāṭṭukkaruvikaḷ muliyavaṟṟic cumēriyāvukku ēṟṟumati ceytaṉar.
(10) pāracīka vaḷikuṭāviliruntu maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu pūcappaṭṭa ceṅkāvip poṭi cintu veḷiyil iṟakkumati ceyyappaṭṭatu.
(11) kirēkka ciluvi poṟikkappaṭṭa muttirikaḷ cintuveḷiyiliruntē ayal nāṭukaṭku aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa.
(12) iraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷilum kāṇappaṭṭa eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷ ēṟakkuṟiya oṉṟākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa.
(13) ekiptil cila pīṭaṅkaḷiṉ kālkaḷ erutiṉ kālkaḷippōla irukkiṉṟaṉa. mohañco-tarōvil ittakiya pīṭaṅkaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.
iṉṉa piṟa cāṉṟukaḷāl, cintu veḷi makkaḷ mēṟku nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ tari vaḻiyākavum, kaṭal vaḻiyākavum vāṇipam naṭatti vantaṉar eṉpati naṉkaṟiyalām.
uḷnāṭṭu vāṇipattiṉ oru pakuti cintu āṟṟiṉ mūlamākavē naṭantatu. appaṇṭik kālattil orēvita nākarikattil irunta cintu veḷi nakaraṅkaḷ pala, cintu āṟṟiṉ vaḻiyē tamakkuḷ vāṇipam naṭattikkoṇṭaṉa; arapikkaṭal tuṟimukap paṭṭiṉaṅkaṭkut tam poruḷkaḷip paṭakukaḷil ēṟṟic cintu āṟṟiṉ mūlam aṉuppiṉa; mēṟkuppuṟa nāṭukaḷi liruntu tuṟimukap paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷi aṭinta poruḷkaḷi muṉ coṉṉa paṭakukaḷ mūlamē peṟṟuk koṇṭaṉa.
paṇṭi makkaḷ, ivvāṟu cintu yāṟṟuvaḻiyē vanta poruḷkaḷik kappalkaḷil ēṟṟik koṇṭu tuṟimukaṅ kaḷi viṭṭuk kari ōramākavē teṉ palucittāṉam, teṉ pāracīkam, irāk, apicīṉiyā, ekiptu eṉṉum nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ vāṇipam ceytaṉarātal vēṇṭum. appaṇṭi makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya muttiri oṉṟil kappal paṭam oṉṟum, pāṉimītu ōṭattiṉ paṭam oṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ippaṭaṅkaḷāl kaṭaliṟ cellum kappalkaḷiyum āṟṟil cellum ōṭaṅkaḷiyum naṉku aṟintu payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpati aṟiyalām.
cintu veḷikku `jēṭ eṉṉum oruvakik kal naṭu āciyāviliruntu vantatu. akkal cintu veḷi mūlam teṉ pāracīkam, irāk, cumēriyā, ciriyā ekiptu nāṭukaṭku aṉuppappaṭṭatu. eṉavē, akkālat tari vāṇipam ēṟakkuṟiya 3000 mil tolivil naṭantu vantatu eṉpatu aṟiyat takkatu. ittarivaḻi vāṇipattiṟku appaṇṭi makkaḷ erutukaḷiyum kōvēṟu kaḻutikaḷiyum payaṉ paṭuttiṉar. avaṟṟuḷḷum kaḻutikaḷiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. avaṟṟuḷḷum erutukaḷē mikutiyākap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, erutukaḷē appaṇṭikkāla vāṇipatti vaḷarttaṉa eṉṉalām.
paṇṭi makkaḷ vāṇipattiṉ poruṭṭuk kiyāṇṭa niṟik kaṟkaḷ kuṟippiṭat takkavi. ivi aṉittum vaḻavaḻappuṭaṉum paḷapaḷappuṭaṉum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nīḷam, akalam, uyaram ākiya mūṉṟu aḷavukaḷum orē muṟiyil irukkumpaṭi ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷa kaṟkaḷē pala. ivi mikac ciṟiya aḷavu mutal palvēṟu niṟikaḷik kuṟippaṉavāka iruk kiṉṟaṉa. cila viyattaṟkuriya periya aḷavik kuṟippaṉa. cila uruṇṭu nīṇṭaviyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. vēṟu cila niṟikaḷ iṉṟiya viṟakuk kaṭikaḷil uḷḷa niṟikaḷip pōlap peruttirukkiṉṟaṉa. avi kūmpiya ucciyi uṭiyaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu 25 pavuṇṭukkum mēṟpaṭṭa niṟi uṭiyatu. in niṟikkaṟkaḷ yāvum karuṅkal, mākkal, paḷiṅkuk kal, paccik kal, maṅkiya civappuk kal mutaliya palatiṟappaṭṭa kaṟkaḷāl aḻakuṟac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
niṟi aḷavukaḷ kuṟippiṭat takkavi. avi 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 160, 200, 320,640, 1600, 3200, 6400, 8000, 128000 ākiya eṇkaḷik kuṟippaṉa. oṉṟu eṉpatu 0.8565 kirām ākum. `inta aḷavikaḷ aṉittum cintuveḷikkē uriyavi. ivaṟṟik kāṇa, cintuveḷi makkaḷ vāṇipattiṟ ciṟanta perumakkaḷ eṉpatil aiyamilli eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar aṟikiṉṟaṉar. tarācut taṭṭukaḷ cempālum, taṇṭu veṇkalattālum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa pōlum! aḷavu kōl iruntatu. muttiri patikkum paḻakkamum akkālattil iruntatu.
ituvari kūṟivanta ceytikaḷāl, cintu veḷiyil vāḻnta nammuṉṉōr, intiyā muḻuvatilum tari vaḻiyāka vāṇipatti naṭatti vantaṉar eṉpatum tari vaḻiyākavum kaṭal vaḻiyākavum ekiptu variyil veḷināṭṭu vāṇipattit tiṟampaṭa naṭatti vantaṉar eṉpatum, takka niṟikaḷiyum  piṟa aḷavikaḷiyum muttirikaḷiyum peṟṟiruntaṉar eṉpatum naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa allavā?
7. kalikaḷ

mohañco-tarōvil vāḻviṟkuriya kalikaḷ ellām iruntaṉa. eḻuttukkaḷ iruntamiyāl ammakkaḷ moḻi uṇarcci uṭiyavar eṉṉalām; mēlum, muttiri kaḷil palakatikaḷik kuṟippiṭum campavaṅkaḷ poṟikkap paṭṭiruttalāl, ilakkiyak kali uṇarcci uṭiyavar eṉṉal tavaṟākātu. aṉṟu cintuveḷiyil, tōṉṟiya kāviyak kali uṇarcciyē, piṟkāla irāmāyaṇa - pārataṅ kaḷil kāṇappaṭum kāviya iṉpattiṟku aṭippaṭiyākum eṉpatil tavaṟilli. eṉavē, cintuveḷi makkaḷ ilakkiya uṇarcci koṇṭa nākarika makkaḷē eṉpatu poruntum.
kāviyak kali uṇarcci aṟṟa makkaḷukku ōviyak kali uṇarcci tōṉṟātu. cintu veḷi makkaḷ pāṉi ōṭukaḷ mītum ōviyaṅkaḷ tiṟampaṭattīṭṭi iruttalik kāṇa - ituvari kiṭittuḷḷa 1000-kku mēṟpaṭṭa muttirikaḷilum ōviyaṅkaḷ cetukkiyuḷḷati nōkka-avartam ōviyakkali aṟivi nām eḷitil uṇarakkūṭum. ōviyam kāviyam pōṉṟatē. kāviyam uḷḷattik koṇṭu paṭittu uṇarappaṭuvatu; ōviyam kaṇkoṇṭu kāṇappaṭuvatu. nūlil uḷḷatic cittira vaṭivil kāṭṭalē ōviyakkali. eṉavē, kāviyakkali uḷḷa iṭattētāṉ ōviyakkali uṇṭākum.
mohañco-tarōviṟ kiṭittuḷḷa ōviyaṅkaḷ, mikat teḷivāka akkāla niliyiyum, nākarikattiyum uṇartta vallaviyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. avi piṟkāla ōviyak kaliyiṉ piṟappiṭam eṉṉalām. oru vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ pala vaṭṭaṅkaḷ piḻipaṭāmal iṭṭuḷḷa muṟi pōṟṟutaṟku uriyatu. pala pāṇṭaṅkaḷmītu maraṅkaḷ, ilikaḷ, ilik kottukaḷ, carutak kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ, kuṟukkum neṭukkumāka uḷḷa mukkōṇaṅkaḷ, paṟavikaḷ, pāmpukaḷ, mīṉkaḷ, maliyāṭukaḷ, kāṭṭu erumikaḷ muliyavaṟṟiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ cevviyākat tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ari aṅkulattiṟkum uṭpaṭṭa aḷavuṭiya muttirikaḷmītum aḻakiya ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭum toḻil nipuṇar akkālattē iruntaṉar eṉpatu viyappiṉum viyappaṉṟō!
cintuveḷi makkaḷ ciṟpak kaliyiyum vaḷarttavar āvar. mohañco-tarōviṟ kiṭitta cuṇṇāmpukkal koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭa cili oṉṟē avartam nuṇṇaṟivit teḷḷitil uṇarttap pōtumāṉatu. avvuruvam yōki allatu camayat talivar uruvam ākum. atu cittirat tiyalik koṇṭa pōrvi oṉṟip pōrttuḷḷatu; mīci iṉṟit tāṭi maṭṭum vittuḷḷatu; attāṭi oḻuṅkāka amikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu; talimayir oḻuṅkākac cīvappeṟṟu vaṭṭap patakkattuṭaṉ kūṭiya tali aṇiyāl aḻuttap paṭṭuḷḷatu. icciliyik kaṇṭu ārāycci yāḷar aticayikkiṉṟaṉar.
harappāvil cila kaṟcilikaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avi viyakkattakka muṟiyil vaḻavaḻappākac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kikaḷ taṉiyē ceytu iṇippataṟku ēṟṟavāṟu accili kaḷil tuḷikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avvāṟē talikaḷum iṇikkappaṭṭaṉa. kaṟkaḷil iṇippu vēliyic ceyyattuṇinta ap pēraṟiñartam ciṟpakkali aṟivi eṉṉeṉpatu! inta āṭavar cilikaḷ, maṇaṟ kallālum, paḻuppu niṟak kaṟkaḷālum ceyyappaṭṭavi.
veṇkalattāl ceyyappaṭṭa naṭaṉap peṇ uruvam oṉṟum cintuveḷi makkaḷatu kali aṟivik kāṭṭuvatākum. veṇkala vārppaṭam koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭa avvuruvam, vaḻa vaḻappākavum oḻuṅkākavum amintuḷḷatu. atu naṭaṉam ceyyat tayārākavuḷḷa oruttiyi uṇarttuvatākum. vēṟu cila naṭaṉac cilikaḷ iṭakkālit tūkki valakkālmītu niṉṟu naṭaṉam ceyvatu pōla amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ic cilikaḷ civa perumāṉatu piṟkāla naṭaṉattiṟkut tōṟṟuvāy ākalām eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu.
ciṟpakkali kirēkkariṭamiruntē intiyāviṟku vantatu eṉṟu ituvari palarum nampiṉar. atu tavaṟu eṉpatiyum, intiyāvilēyē ciṟpakkali taṉi muṟiyil tōṉṟi vaḷarcci peṟṟatu; atu varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟkum muṟpaṭṭatu eṉpatiyum cintuveḷic cilikaḷ meyppittuviṭṭaṉa.
cila cittiraṅkaḷik koṇṭu pārkkiyil, akkāla makkaḷ tavul, mirutaṅkam, vīṇi, tāḷaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟik kiyāṇṭaṉar eṉpati uṇaralām. eṉavē, avarkaḷ icik kali uṇarcciyi uṭiyavarākavē iruntaṉar eṉpatu tēṟṟam, naṭaṉa mātiṉ ciliyiyum vēṟu cila naṭaṉac cili kaḷiyum nōkka, appaṇṭi makkaḷ naṭaṉakkali aṟiviyum peṟṟiruntaṉar eṉpatu teḷivākum.
cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭittuḷḷa niṟik kaṟkaḷ perumpālum ellā vīṭukaḷilum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ mikak kuṟinta aḷavu tacāmica piṉṉattiṟ celkiṟatu; pēraḷavu 15 pavuṇṭ vari celkiṟatu. itaṉāl, akkāla makkaḷatu kaṇitap pulami naṉku teḷivākiṟataṉṟō? `eṇṇum eḻuttum kaṇṇeṉat takum eṉpati avarkaḷ naṉku aṟintu vāḻntaṉar.
cintuveḷi makkaḷ maruttuvak kaliyilum ōraḷavu tērcci peṟṟavar āvar. ikkāla āyurvēta yūṉāṉi maruttuvap pulavar kiyāḷum `cilā cittu eṉṉum maruntu mohañco-tarōviṟ kiṭittuḷḷatu. `cilā cittu eṉpatu vayiṟṟil uṇṭākum kuṭalippaṟṟiya - nuriyīralip paṟṟiya-nōykaḷik kuṇappaṭuttum āṟṟal peṟṟatu. aḵtu, imayamalip pāṟikaḷil kacintu varuvatu. ataṉi malināṭṭār koṇṭuvantu koṭuttaṉar; iṉṟum koṭukkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ camparmāṉ kompukaḷip poṭiyākki maruntākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpatum terikiṟatu. māṭṭuk kompukaḷ kiṇṇampōlak kuṭiyappaṭṭuḷḷati nōkka, avi maruntuvikkap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa vātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu aṟiyakkiṭakkiṟatu.
cintuveḷi makkaḷ vīṭukaḷ kaṭṭiyuḷḷa muṟiyiliruntu irāci kaṇaṅkaḷiṉ iyakkaṅkaḷik kavaṉittu vantavar eṉpati aṟiyalām. `avarkaḷ kālattil irāci maṇṭalattil eṭṭu irācikaḷē iruntaṉa. akkālam ēṟakkuṟiya ki. mu. 5610 eṉak kūṟalām. atu cumēriyar nākarikamē tōṉṟāta kālam eṉṟu hīrā pātiriyār kūṟiyuḷḷār.
kittoḻiṟ kalikaḷil cintuveḷi makkaḷ mikka tērcci peṟṟavar eṉpatu muṉ pakutiyil viḷakkappeṟṟatu. avarkaḷ uṭaṟ payiṟciyilum ōraḷavu payiṟci peṟṟavar eṉpatu cila muttirikaḷil uḷḷa ōviyaṅkaḷik koṇṭu kūṟalām.
mohañco-tarō ciṟanta vāṇipattalam ātalāl, nakareṅkum kāvaṟ kūṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. aṅkirunta kāvalāḷikaḷ kaḷvarip piṭikkattakka aṟivu naṉku peṟṟavarātal vēṇṭum. iṉṟēl, nakaram naṉṉiliyil iruttal iyalātaṉṟō?
ituvari kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, mohañco-tarō, harappā mutaliya cintuveḷi nakaraṅkaḷil vāḻnta nam muṉṉōr, vāṇipattāl ciṟappuṟṟuk kit toḻililum ciṟappaṭintu, ici, naṭaṉam, ciṟpam, ōviyam, kaṇitam mutaliya palavakik kalikaḷil-vāḻkki iṉpattiṟkut tēviyāṉa kalikaḷil-payiṟci peṟṟu iṉpa vāḻvu vāḻntaṉar eṉpatu veḷḷiṭi malipōl viḷakka mātal kāṇka. iṉi, aṭutta pakutiyil avartam camayak kali aṟivik kāṇpōm.
8. camayakkali

cintuveḷiyil kaṇimaṇṇāṟ ceytu cuṭap peṟṟa ciṟiya vaṭivaṅkaḷum, muttirikaḷum, ulōkat takaṭukaḷum tāyittukkaḷum nirampak kiṭittaṉa: avaṟṟik koṇṭu ak kālattavaruṭiya kaṭavuḷ vaṇakkattiṟku uriya poruḷkaḷ iṉṉavi eṉpati oruvāṟu aṟiyalām.
ulakattup paṇṭi makkaḷ, tamakkup palavaki uṇavup poruḷkaḷit tarum tariyit tāyāka matittu vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. avar pōlavē cintuveḷi makkaḷum tarip peṇ (nilamakkaḷ) vaṇakkam koṇṭiruntaṉar; avvammikku āṭu, kōḻi mutaliyavaṟṟip paliyiṭṭu vantaṉar. attēvati nirampa nakikaḷi aṇintiruntatu; taliyil viciṟip pākiyi uṭiyatu.
cintuveḷi makkaḷiṭam civa vaṇakkamē ciṟappākak kuṭikoṇṭu iruntatu eṉpatik kuṟippaṉa pōlak kaṇakkaṟṟa iliṅkaṅkaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. iṭikkālit tūkki āṭum, naṭarācar cili pōṉṟatu kiṭittuḷḷatu. oru muttiriyil maṉita uruvam oṉṟu yōkattil iruppatu pōlap poṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. mārpil mukkōṇa vaṭivap patakkaṅkaḷ kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. kikaḷ niyik kaṭakaṅkaḷ pūṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. iṭuppil iraṭṭip paṭṭiyāka ōr arikkacci kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. iv vuruvattiṉ valappuṟam yāṉiyum puliyum iṭappuṟam erutum kāṇṭā mirukamum niṟkiṉṟaṉa. pīṭattiṉ aṭiyil iraṇṭu māṉkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. yōkiyiṉ talimītu vaḷinta erumik kompukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. `ivvuruvam civaṉik kuṟippatu; talimītuḷḷa kompukaḷum pūṅkottum tiricūlattik kuṟippaṉa; vilaṅkukaḷ pakkattil iruttal, civaṉ - pacupati eṉpatik kuṟippatu. mūṉṟu talikaḷ naṉku terivatāl, piṉpakkam iraṇṭu talikaḷ irukkum eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar aṟikiṉṟaṉar.
iliṅkaṅkaḷ palavaṭiviṉavākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. harappāvil maṭṭum 600-kku mēṟpaṭṭa civaliṅkaṅkaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. paṇṭi makkaḷ ciṟiya iliṅkaṅkaḷit tāyittukkaḷ pōlak kaḻuttilō kiyilō kaṭṭi iruntavarātal vēṇṭum; iviyaṉṟi, muttirikaḷum cempu villikaḷ pōṉṟaṉavum aṇiyappaṭṭu vantaṉa.
oru muttiriyil ōr uruvam uṭkārntirukkiṟatu; ataṉ irupuṟamum variciyākac cilar niṉṟu vaṇaṅkukiṉṟaṉar; pattar talimītu paṭam virittāṭum pāmpu pōṉṟa taliyaṇikaḷ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. ikkāṭci, puttar perumāṉi nāka aracarkaḷ vaṇaṅkuvatu pōṉṟa kāṭciyi niṉippūṭṭukiṟatu. `kaṇṇapirāṉ kaliyaṉ eṉṉum nāka aracaṉi veṉṟa piṟaku avaṉum avaṉic cērntavarum kaṇṇaṉi vaṇaṅkum kāṭciyē atu eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. iḵtu uṇmiyāyiṉ, ap paḻaṅkālattilēyē viṇavamum innāṭṭil iruntatu eṉpatu pulaṉākum.
kompuḷḷa teyvaṅkaḷ, nāṟṟit teyvaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavi cila muttirikaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vēṟu cilavaṟṟil aṟuvar niṉṟu yōkam ceyvatupōla ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. `niṟkum niliyil yōkam purital camaṇarkkē ciṟappāṉatu. mēlum ivi vaṭa maturiyil uḷḷa camaṇar cilikaḷi ottuḷḷaṉa. erutu camaṇaratu aṭiyāḷak kuṟi ākum. cila muttirikaḷil yōkiyiṉ muṉpuṟam erutu iruttal kuṟippiṭattakkatu. ākavē, ivvuruvam iṭapa tēvarik kuṟippatākalām eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar cilar aṟivar; itaṉiyē vēṟu cilar, `civaperumāṉik kuṟippatu eṉpar.
cūriya vaṇakkamum mohañco-tarōvil iruntatu. kalappu uruvaṅkaḷ pala, vaṇakkam peṟṟiruntaṉa. maṉitat tali, yāṉi uṭal, erutiṉ kompukaḷ, puliyiṉ kālkaḷ, puli vāl ikkūṭṭu uruvam oṉṟu teyvamāka vaṇaṅkap peṟṟatu. erutuk kompukaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya maṉita uruvam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ataṉ kālkaḷ erutiṉ kālkaḷ ākum. ittakiya vinōta uruvaṅkaḷ cumēriyāvil nimpak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. im maṉitar `teyva cakti vāyntavar eṉpatu appaṇṭi makkaḷ eṇṇam. peṇṇum puliyum cērnta uruvam oṉṟum kiṭittatu. puliyi oru tēvatiyākap paḻiya makkaḷ eṇṇiṉar pōlum! kompu muḷitta pāmpukaḷ vaṇaṅkap peṟṟaṉa. karuṭap paṟavikaḷ vaṇaṅkap peṟṟaṉa.
mara vaṇakkam cintu veḷiyil ciṟappāka iruntatu. maraṅkaḷiṉ aṭiyil mēṟcoṉṉa palavaki vinōta uruvaṅkaḷ, iruppatākac cila muttirikaḷil poṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. marattiṉ aṭiyil āṭu mutaliyavaṟṟi mara tēvatikaṭkup pali iṭutal cila muttirikaḷāl pulaṉākiṟatu. mara tēvatikaḷ iṉṟum tamiḻ makkaḷāl vaṇakkam peṟutal īṇṭu niṉikkattakkatu. āṟṟu vaṇakkamum, akkālattil iruntatu. iv vaṇakkam iṉṟum `patiṉeṭṭām perukkam, eṉṉum peyaril namatu tamiḻ nāṭṭil iruppatiyum ciṟappāka intiyā muḻuvatilum iruppatiyum aṟika.
camaya campantamākat tāyittu aṇital akkāla vaḻakkam. ivvaḻakkam iṉṟum naṭimuṟiyil irukkiṉṟataṉṟō? aṇil, cemmaṟiyāṭu muyal, māṉ, nāy ākiya vilaṅkukaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ - kal, pīṅkāṉ, veṇkalam cuṭaṭa kaḷimaṇ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ villikaḷil poṟikkappaṭṭu mēlē tuḷi yiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi kayiṟu koṇṭu kaṭṭikkoḷḷap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. talippil muṭiyiṭappeṟṟa cippiyālāṉa tāyittu oṉṟu mantira catti uṭiyatākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. cila tāyittukkaḷil cuvatikā, kirēkkacciluvi pōṉṟa kuṟikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
camaya campantamāṉa patakkaṅkaḷum paṇṭik kālac cintuveḷi makkaḷāl aṇiyappaṭṭu vantaṉa. viḻākkaḷ naṭipeṟṟaṉa; kaṭavuḷ uruvaṅkaḷ ūr valamāka eṭuttuc cellappaṭṭaṉa; camaya campantamāka iciyum naṭaṉamum vaḻakkil iruntaṉa. mohañco-tarōvil kōvilkaḷ iruntiruttal, vēṇṭum. karuppavatikaḷik kuṟikkum patumikaḷ, kikkuḻantikaḷi ēnti niṟkum tāy mārik kuṟikkum patumikaḷ tavaḻntu cellum kuḻantikaḷik kuṟikkum patumikaḷ mutaliyavaṉavum vilaṅkup patumikaḷum pirārttaṉiyiṉ poruṭṭuk kōvilkaḷil vikkap peṟṟaṉavāka iruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar niṉikkiṉṟaṉar. ippaḻakkam tamiḻariṭiyē iṉṟaḷavum iruntu varukiṉṟatu.
curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, cintuveḷi makkaḷ, (1) tarippeṇ vaṇakkam (2) iliṅka vaṇakkam (3) civa vaṇakkam (4) nanti vaṇakkam (5) cūriya vaṇakkam (6) vilaṅku vaṇakkam (7) paṟavi vaṇakkam (8) nāka vaṇakkam (9) mara vaṇakkam (10) āṟṟu vaṇakkam (11) ciṟu tey vaṇakkam (12) vinōta tey vaṇakkam (13) kaṇṇaṉ vaṇakkam mutaliyavaṟṟi uṭiyavar eṉṟu kūṟalām. ivaṟṟōṭu avarkaḷ, (1) camayat toṭarpāṉa tāyittukkaḷiyum muttirikaḷiyum patakkaṅkaḷiyum aṇintu vantārkaḷ, (2) paliyiṭṭu vantārkaḷ, (3) palavakip poruḷkaḷip paṭittu vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ, (4) kaṭavuḷarkku viḻākkaḷi naṭatti vantārkaḷ, (5) pirārttaṉi celutti vantārkaḷ, (6) camayat toṭarpāka iciyum naṭaṉamum vaḷarttārkaḷ, (7) mantira māyaṅkaḷil paḻakiṉārkaḷ, (8) yōkap payiṟciyiyum naṉku aṟintu paḻaki iruntārkaḷ eṉpavaṟṟiyum oruvāṟu uṇaralām.
curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, iṉṟu intu matattil uḷḷa perumpālāṉa vaḻipāṭukaḷum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷum cintu veḷi makkaḷuṭiya camayattaṉavē ākum. eṉakkūṟal tavaṟākātu.
9. iṭutalum cuṭutalum

kirī, ekiptu mutaliya paṇṭi nāṭukaḷil muṟkālattil iṟantārip putittu vantaṉar; pāracīkattil piṇaṅkaḷi eṟintu vilaṅkukaṭkum paṟavikaṭkum iriyākkiṉar. tipēttil iṟantār uṭalip pala pākaṅkaḷāka veṭṭip paṉṟikaṭkum nāykaṭkum pōṭṭaṉar; iṉṟum pōṭu kiṉṟaṉar. paṇṭit tamiḻ nāṭṭil periya tāḻikaḷil iṟantārip putittu vantaṉar; maṇ peṭṭikaḷi iraṇṭākat taṭuttu, oru pātiyil iṟantavar uṭamiyiyum, maṟṟoru pātiyil avar payaṉpaṭuttiya karuvikaḷiyum vittu aṭakkam ceytaṉar. ittakiya tāḻikaḷ pala pallāvaram, putukkōṭṭi, āticca nallūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. iṉṉum veḷiyil eṭukkappaṭāta tāḻikaḷ āyirakkaṇakkil putukkōṭṭic cīmiyil irukkiṉṟaṉa.
iṅṅaṉam putitta iṭaṅkaḷ mītu aṭiyāḷak kuṟikaḷ vittalō, kaṭṭalō paḻiya vaḻakkam. ekiptiya aracarkaḷip putitta iṭaṅkaḷil kaṇṇiyum karuttiyum kavaravalla piramiṭ kōpuraṅkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. avi 5000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. āyiṉum, iṉṟum putiyaṉavākak kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. puttaratu uṭali erittu, cāmpaliyum paṟkaḷiyum aracar palar paṅkiṭṭuk koṇṭaṉar; tattam nāṭukaḷil avaṟṟi aṭakkam ceytu, avviṭaṅkaḷil vāṉaḷāvum kōvilkaḷik kaṭṭiṉar.
ittakiya kōvilkaḷō piṟa ciṉṉaṅkaḷō cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭittil; iṭukāṭō-cuṭukāṭō kiṭittila. āyiṉ, cantukaḷil, vīṭukaṭku aṭiyil nīr aruntum kuvaḷikaṭkuḷ irunta eritta cāmpalum elumput tuṇṭukaḷum ērāḷamākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟi nōkka, cintuveḷi makkaḷ iṟantavar uṭali erittuc cāmpaliyum elumpukaḷi yum cēmittup putittu vantaṉar eṉpatu terikiṟatu. ip paḻakkam iṉṟum nammiṭi iruntuvarutal kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
harappāvil kiṭitta cavak kuḻikaḷil iraṇṭu mūṉṟu aṭukkukaḷ uṇṭu. mikavum aṭiyil irunta cavakkuḻikaḷil piṇaṅkaḷi nērākap paṭukkavittuk kikaḷi mārpiṉmītu maṭakki vittup putittu vantaṉar eṉpatu terikiṟatu. nilattiṟku aṭiyil putikkappaṭṭa uṭalkaḷ mūṉṟu vakiyāka aṭakkam ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa: (1) cila uṭalkaḷ taṉittaṉiyē iṭamviṭṭup putikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. (2) cila nerukkamākap putikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa; (3) cila oṉṟaṉkīḻ oṉṟākap putikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ippaḻakkam palucittāṉattilum peru vaḻakkuṭiyatāka iruntatu. avvuṭalkaḷiṉ arukil nīruṭiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, kōppikaḷ, taṭṭukaḷ iṟantār payaṉpaṭuttiya piṟaporuḷkaḷ mutaliyaṉa putikkappaṭṭaṉa.
harappāvil eṭukkappaṭṭa tāḻikaḷ ellām ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappeṟṟaṉa. ovvoṉṟiṉ mēlum vita vitamāṉa ōviyaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oru tāḻiyiṉ kaḻuttarukil karuṭap paṟavikaḷ iraṇṭuvaricikaḷil paṟappaṉa pōlat tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; avvaricikaṭku iṭiyē ilikkottukaḷ kāṇappaṭum toṭṭikaḷ aṇiyaṇiyāka vikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. piṟa tāḻikaḷmītu, cempaṭavaṉ vali pōṭṭu mīṉ piṭippatu pōlavum, mīṉ - āmi - paṟavikaḷ - ilikaḷ mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷip pōlavum cittiraṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
akkāla makkaḷ mayilkaḷit teyvattaṉmi uṭiyaviyāka matittaṉar. im mayilkaḷiṉ tuṇiyik koṇṭu, maṉitar āvikaḷ mēlulakam pōvaṉ eṉpati akkālattavar eṇṇi vantaṉarō - allatu cūkkum uṭalē mayilāka uruvakappaṭuttap paṭṭatō, teriyavilli. oru tāḻi mītuḷḷa cittiraṅkaḷil mūṉṟu mayilkaḷ paṟappaṉappōlat tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. am mayilkaṭku naṭuvil viṇmīṉkaḷ variyap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ātaliṉ, `mayilkaḷ cūkkum uṭalkaḷik kuṟippaṉa; avi mēl ulakatti aṭivatākappaṇṭiyōr eṇṇiṉar, eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar aṟikiṉṟaṉar. cila tāḻikaḷmītu ciṟiya mayilkaḷ aṇiyaṇiyāka iruppaṉapōla ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. vēṟu cilavaṟṟiṉmītu aintu mayilkaḷ paṟappaṉapōlak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cilatāḻikaḷ mītu kāṇappaṭum mayilkaḷiṉ tōki tiricūlam pōlak kāṇap paṭukiṟatu. cila mayilkaḷiṉ tali mītu iraṇṭu kompukaḷum avaṟṟukku iṭiyil nimirnta malarkkottum kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa.
cila tāḻikaḷmītu paṟavimūkkuṭiya maṉita uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi, mayilkaḷ variyappaṭṭa karuttuk koṇṭē variyappaṭṭavi; aḵtāvatu, `cūkkum uṭampic cumantu cellumvaṉmi vāyntavi ākalām eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. oru tāḻimītu paṟavimūkkuṭiya maṉita uruvam tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. avvuruvam iṭakkiyil ampum villum vittuḷḷatu. ataṉ irupuṟamum iraṇṭu erutukaḷ niṟkiṉṟaṉa. avverutukaḷik kayiṟukoṇṭu kaṭṭi, avaṟṟiṉ kayiṟṟi maṉita vuruvam taṉ valakkiyil piṭittuḷḷatu. iṭappuṟamuḷḷa eruti nāy oṉṟu turattivantu, vālip piṭittuk kaṭittu iḻukkiṉṟatu. nāykkup piṉ, talimītu kompu muḷitta mayilkaḷ iraṇṭu paṟakkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṟku arukil periya veḷḷāṭu oṉṟu niṟkiṟatu. ataṉ kompukaḷ 8 tiricūlaṅ kaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu pala uruvaṅkaḷik kuṟikkum ic cittiram, `yamaṉ, uyirik kavarntu celvatik kuṟippatākalām,  eṉṟu aṟiñar niṉik kiṉṟaṉar.
iṟantavar aṇintirunta aṇikaḷum, payaṉpaṭuttiya pāṇṭaṅkaḷum, piṟa poruḷkaḷum uṇavup poruḷkaḷum tāḻikaḷil iruntu eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟi ellām iṟantavar cāmpaluṭaṉ tāḻikaḷil iṭṭup putittuviṭal appaṇṭi makkaḷ paḻakkam eṉpatu terikiṟatu. tāḻikaḷil cūḷi iṭappeṟṟa maṇ appaṅkaḷ kiṭittatik kuṟippākak kūṟalām. pala tāḻikaḷil taṅka nakikaḷ akappaṭṭaṉa: navarattiṉaṅkaḷ kiṭittaṉa. ivvāṟu tāḻikaḷil iṟantār aṇikaḷip putittal paṇṭi mēṟkuppuṟa nāṭukaḷilum iruntu vanta paḻakkamē ākum.
oru piṇak kuḻiyiṟ kiṭitta muttiri oṉṟil, kaṭṭilmītu maṅki orutti cāyntukoṇṭu iruppatupōla uruvam cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉāl, `iṟanta āṭavaṉukku uriya maṉivi kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ iṟakkum vaḻakkam akkālattil iruntirukkalām. eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar; `iṟantavar, aṭutta vāḻkkiyil ippiṟaviyil iruntapaṭiyē irukka virumpiṉar; av viruppattāṟṟāṉ maṉiviyum uṭaṉ iṟantavaḷātal vēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar.
mohañco-tarō, harappā mutaliyaṉa ciṟanta nākarikamum celvap perukkamum uṭiya nakaraṅkaḷ; vāṇipap pukaḻpeṟṟa nakaraṅkaḷ, ātaliṉ, avaṟṟil pala nāṭṭumakkaḷ kuṭi pukuntaṉar; avaravar tattam nāṭṭup paḻakkattiṟku ēṟpavē iṟantār uṭalip putittum, erittum nāy narikaṭku iṭṭum vantaṉar eṉpati harappāvil kiṭitta putikuḻikaḷik koṇṭu aṟiyalām. ip palavaki aṭakka muṟikaḷ iṉṟum ceṉṉi, kalkattā, pampāy pōṉṟa vāṇipap perukkam koṇṭa nakaraṅkaḷil-pala nāṭṭumakkaḷ vacikkiṉṟa nakaraṅkaḷil - kāṇalām. eṉiṉum, cintuveḷi makkaṭkē ciṟappāka irunta paḻakkam iṭutalum cuṭutalumē ākum eṉpatu, mohañco-tarōvil vīṭukaṭkaṭiyil kiṭitta cāmpal koṇṭa maṭkalaṅkaḷālum, harappāvil kiṭitta muḻuvuṭal puti muṟiyālum naṉkaṟiyalām. mēlum, cintuveḷi makkaḷ maṟu piṟavu uṇarcci uṭiyavarāka iruntaṉar eṉpatum aṟiyattakkatu.
10. cintuveḷi eḻuttukaḷ

cintuveḷiyiṟ kiṭitta muttirikaḷil periṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷa cittirak kuṟikaḷē cintu makkaḷ moḻikkuriya eḻuttukkaḷ. avaṟṟi vakippaṭutti, `cintuveḷi eḻuttuk kaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal eṉṟum `paṇṭi intiya eḻuttukkaḷiṉ amippu muṟi eṉṟum, `cintuveḷi eḻuttukaḷ eṉṟum aṟiñarkaḷ, tāmtām aṟintavari veḷippaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷukkup piṟaku ṭākṭar haṉṭṭar eṉṉum pēraṟiñar virivāka ārāyntu aḻakiya nūl oṉṟi 1934-l veḷi yiṭṭār.
inta eḻuttukaḷ paṭikkamuṭiyāta niliyil irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivi paṭikkappaṭṭa piṟakē cintu veḷi makkaḷiṉ uṇmi varalāṟṟi uḷḷavāṟu uṇarakkūṭum; ittakiya eḻuttukaḷ cumēriyāvilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ekiptiyap paḻiya eḻuttukkaḷum, piṟa mēlnāṭukaḷil kiṭitta eḻuttukaḷum ciṟitu vēṟupāṭṭuṭaṉ peritum ottuḷḷaṉa. ivi aṉittum oru potu eḻuttu muṟiyiliruntu neṭuṅ kālattiṟku muṉṉarē pirintaṉavātal vēṇṭum eṉṟu ṭākṭar haṉṭṭar karutukiṟār.
`ṭeṉaṭaṭ eṉṉum oruvakik kalmītu cuṇṇam taṭavic cuṭṭu, ataṉmītu eḻuttukkaḷum vilaṅku mutaliya uruvaṅkaḷum poṟikkappap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ik kaṟkāḷākiya poruḷkaḷ, caturamākavum nīḷ caturamākavum nīṇṭu uruṇṭa vaṭivamākavum nīḷ caturamākavum nīṇṭu uruṇṭa vaṭivamākavum amintuḷḷaṉa. cila muccaturamāka amintuḷḷaṉa. avi cīṭṭukaḷ (iracītukaḷ) eṉṟu ṭākṭar haṉṭṭar karutukiṟār. vēṟu cila kayiṟṟiṟ kōttuk kaḻuttil kaṭṭikkoḷvaṉa pōla amintuḷḷaṉa. cempālāya nīḷ caturat takaṭukaḷ pala nāṇayaṅkaḷ eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟil vilaṅkiṉ uruvam mēlum eḻuttukkaḷ kīḻumākap poṟikkappap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa cilavaki eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷik koṇṭa poruḷkaḷ oppantac cīṭṭukaḷ eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
cintuveḷi makkaḷ ivaṟṟōṭu niṉṟuviṭavilli. avarkaḷ tōl, paṭṭu, pāpira eṉpavaṟṟiyum payaṉpaṭutti eḻuti irukkalām. eḻuttukaḷ mēliruntu kīḻ nōkki eḻutap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa; nērāka amintuḷḷaṉa. inta eḻuttukkaḷ akara varici uṭiyaṉa alla. ivaṟṟuḷ cittira eḻuttukaḷ pala; olik kuṟippu uṭiyaṉa pala. ivi valam - iṭamāka vācikkappaṭa vēṇṭiyavi; cila iṭaṅkaḷil iṭam - valamāka vācikkat takkapaṭi amintuḷḷaṉa. ivi palavēṟu kālaṅkaḷiṉ vaḷarcci uṭiyaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
inta eḻuttukkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭupavi piṉ varuvaṉa ākum:
1. cintuveḷi eḻuttukaḷ uccarippik kuṟippaṉa.
2. avi olik kuṟippiyum uṇarvuk kuṟippiyum aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭavi.
3. anta aṭippaṭi ki. mu. 3000-kku muṟpaṭṭatu.
4. ivi cumēriyar - ekiptiyar eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷi ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl mikap paḻiya kālattil cittira eḻuttukkaḷi mūlamākakkoṇṭa oru potu moḻiyi liruntē ivi aṉittum pirintaṉavātal vēṇṭum.
5. cintuveḷi makkaḷ arapikkaṭal, ceṅkaṭal, naṭuttarik kaṭal ākiya im mūṉṟaṉ vaḻiyāka mēṟkup puṟa nāṭukaḷōṭu vāṇipam ceytu vantaṉar ātaliṉ, avarkaḷiṭamiruntē cipra tīviṉar, poṉīciyar, capiyar eṉpavarkaḷ cintu veḷi eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷ cilavaṟṟik kaṭaṉ peṟṟirukkalām.
acōkaṉ cācaṉaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum pirāmi eḻuttukaḷ ki. mu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭic cērntavi. `avi iḻantu viṭṭa intiya eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷiliruntē tōṉṟiṉavātal vēṇṭum eṉṟu sar. alekcāṇṭar kaṉṉiṅkām neṭunāṭkaḷukku muṉ karutiṉār. avar karutiyatu cariyē eṉpatip pērāciriyar lāṅṭaṉ uṇarntār; `cintu veḷi eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷiliruntē pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa vātal vēṇṭum eṉṟu pala kāraṇaṅkaḷik kāṭṭi meyppittuḷḷaḷār. cintuveḷi nākarikam ki. mu. 2500-kku muṟpaṭṭatu. pirāmi eḻuttukaḷ ki. mu. 300-kkuc cariyāṉavi. `ivaṟṟukku iṭippaṭṭa kālattu eḻuttukaḷ eṉṟu karutat takka kuṟikaḷik koṇṭa cācaṉam oṉṟu naṭu intiyāvil vikkiramakōl eṉṉum iṭattil kiṭittuḷḷatu. iṉṉum pala cāṉṟukaḷ nāḷaṭivil veḷippaṭalām. putuccēriyi aṭutta arikkamēṭu eṉṉum iṭattil pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. aṅku mēlum cila paḻiya kuṟikaḷ kiṭikkalām.
cintuveḷi eḻuttukkaḷic cōtikkiyil, teḷivāṉa vēṟupaṭṭa 234 eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷ kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. pirāmiyil a, ā, i, ī, u, ū, e, o eṉṉum 8 uyir eḻuttukaḷum 33 mey eḻuttukkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟāl uṇṭāṉa uyirmey eḻuttukkaḷ (33o8=) 264 ākum. ivaṟṟil 50 eḻuttukkaḷukku uriya kuṟikaḷ cintu veḷi eḻuttukaḷil teḷivākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āciya - āṭrēliyā (muṇṭā) moḻikaḷiṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷ cila, cintuveḷi eḻuttuk kaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷ cila pirākūyi moḻi eḻuttukaḷilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. intiyāvil muṇṭā moḻikaḷē tirāviṭa moḻikku muṟpaṭṭavi. ātaliṉ, avi tirāviṭattilum ōraḷavu kalantirukkalām.
cintuveḷi makkaḷ moḻi, ōraci uṭiya coṟ kaḷiyē peritum koṇṭatākam. ammoḻi camakkirutam aṉṟu. āriyar ki. mu. 1200-kku muṉ intiyāviṟ pukavilli. avarkaṭku muṉ cintu veḷiyil iruntavar tirāviṭar ākalām. palucittāṉattil uḷḷa pirākūyi moḻiyil pēraḷavu tirāviṭakkalappu irukkiṟatu; pirākūyi makkaḷ maṇṭi ōṭukaḷi ottavi moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa; cintuveḷi moḻi pirākūyiyip pōlavē ōracic coṟkaḷi uṭiyatāka irukkiṟatu. ivaṟṟāl, tirāviṭa moḻi vaṭa intiyāvil āriyar varukikkumuṉ iruntatu eṉṟu kūṟalām. tirāviṭarē taṅkaḷ kalikaḷiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum āriyariṭam oppaṭittavar ātal vēṇṭum eṉṟu ṭākṭar haṉṭṭar aṟintuḷḷār.
vaṭa pirāmi eḻuttukaḷum teṉ pirāmi eḻuttukaḷum cintuveḷi eḻuttukkaḷiliruntu vaḷarcci peṟṟaviyē ākum. vaṭa pirāmi āriyarālum teṉ pirāmi tirāviṭarālum vaḷarkkappaṭṭavi. vaṭapirāmi āriya moḻikku ēṟṟapaṭi māṟṟi amikkappeṟṟavi. cintuveḷi eḻuttukkaḷik koṇṭa moḻi paḻiya tirāviṭamē ākum eṉṟu hīrā pātiriyār kūṟukiṉṟār.
pirāmi eḻuttu mutalil vaṭamoḻikkāka ēṟpaṭa villi; uyir eḻuttukaḷil a, i, u ākiya mūṉṟukkum atikac ciṟappi aḷippatum, iṉa eḻuttukkaḷip peṟātatum ākiya oru moḻikkeṉa amikkappaṭṭup piṉṉarē vaṭamoḻikku ēṟṟapaṭi putiya kuṟikaḷ uṇṭākkap paṭṭaṉa. intiyāvil irukkum moḻikaḷuḷ iṅṅaṉam iruppatu tamiḻ oṉṟutāṉ. eṉavē, pirāmi mutalil tamiḻukkeṉa aminta eḻuttāka iruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu vēṟu cila moḻi ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
ituvari kūṟiyavaṟṟāl cintuveḷi eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷ paṇṭit tirāviṭa moḻikkākat tirāviṭa makkaḷ tōṟṟuvittaṉavē ākum eṉpati aṟiyalām.
11. cintuveḷi makkaḷ tirāviṭarā?

intiyāvil irunta paḻaṅkuṭikaḷ muṇṭar allatu kolēriyar eṉpavar. avarkaḷi veṉṟu intiyā muḻuvatum paravi iruntavar tirāviṭar. at tirāviṭarkkup piṟakē āriyar vantaṉar. itu carittira āciriyar ellōrum oppukkoṇṭa uṇmi ākum. kolēriyar iṉattavarē kōṇṭar, caravar mutaliya malivāṇar. ivarkaḷ anākarika makkaḷ. ivar iṉattavar āṭrēliyā vari paravi irukkiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē, ivarkaḷ cintuveḷi nākarikattit tōṟṟuvittavar eṉṟu enta aṟiñarum kūṟat tuṇiyār. cintuveḷi nākarikamō āriyaruṭiyataṉṟu eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar palaratu tuṇipu. eṉavē, cintuveḷi nākarikam tirāviṭarkkē uriyatu eṉpatu kūṟāmalē amiyum aṉṟō?
āyiṉ ki. mu. 3000-l tirāviṭa makkaḷ vaṭintiyāvil iruntaṉar eṉpataṟku vēṟu cāṉṟukaḷ irukkumāyiṉ, ik kūṟṟi oruvāṟu oppuk koḷḷalām. ātaliṉ, ac cāṉṟukaḷik kāṇpōm.
(1) āriyar cintu natikkariyil ceyta mutal nūl rik vētam. atil ēṟattāḻa 400 tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ kalantiruk kiṉṟaṉa.
(2) rik vētam iḻittukkūṟum iliṅka vaḻipāṭu tirāviṭaruṭiyatē ākum.
(3) iṉṟu vaṭa intiyāvil uḷḷa ovvoru moḻi yilum tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉ aṭippaṭiyāṉa ilakkaṇam amintirukkiṟatu.
(4) palucittāṉattil uḷḷa pirākūyi moḻiyil tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa; ammoḻiyāḷar paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ pala tirāviṭaruṭiyaṉa; avarkaḷ uruva amippilum tirāviṭari ottuḷḷaṉar.
(5) vaṭa intiyāvil kōṇṭar, kūyiyar, irācmakāliyar, orāoṉar mutaliya malivāṇar tirāviṭac coṟkaḷiyē pēcukiṉṟaṉar.
(6) iṉṟuḷḷa intiya makkaḷ iṉappaṭṭiyal paṭi, ṭekkāṉ, teṉ intiyap pakutikaḷil iruppavar tirāviṭar; pampāy, cintu mākāṇaṅkaḷil iruppavar citiya-tirāviṭar; kiḻakkup pañcāp, aikkiya maṇṭilam, pīkār maṇṭilam ivaṟṟil iruppavar āriya tirāviṭar; vaṅkāḷam, orisā mākāṇaṅkaḷil iruppavar maṅkōliya-tirāviṭar. eṉavē, ēṟakkuṟiya intiyā muḻuvatilum (kāṣmīr, irācaputaṉam tavira) tirāviṭar iṉṟum irukkiṉṟaṉar eṉpatu aṟiyat takkatu. itu kāṟum kūṟiya cāṉṟukaḷālēyē tirāviṭa makkaḷ intiyā muḻuvatilum āriyar varumpoḻutu iruntaṉar eṉpati aṟiyalām. iv vuṇmiyic cintu veḷi ārāycci koṇṭu niṟuvalām:
(1) cintu veḷiyil iliṅka, vaṇakkam iruntatu. cintuveḷi makkaḷ camayap paḻakkaṅkaḷ ellām iṉṟum tirāviṭa makkaḷiṭam-ciṟappākat tamiḻ makkaḷiṭam-iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa.
(2) cintuveḷi vīṭṭu vāyilkaḷ āṟaṭi uyarattiṟku uṭpaṭṭaviyē; tirāviṭar uyaramum āṟaṭikku uṭpaṭṭatē.
(3) teṉṉintiya tirāviṭar ki. mu. 4000lēyē cumēriyaruṭaṉ kaṭal vāṇipam ceytavar; cintuveḷi makkaḷum cumēriyaruṭaṉ kaṭal vāṇipam ceytavar.
(4) tirunelvēli, nīlakiri, hitarāpāt camattāṉam ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭṭa cittira eḻuttukaḷē cintu veḷiyilum kāṇappaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷ ākum.
(5) cintu veḷiyil kāṇappaṭṭa maṇṭi ōṭukaḷ cila pallāvaram, āticca nallūr ivaṟṟiṟ kiṭitta maṇṭi ōṭukaḷi ottuḷḷaṉa.
(6) cintuveḷi makkaḷ mēlāṭi pōrttiruntatu pōlavē iṉṟum tirāviṭar pōrttiruppatu kāṇalām.
(7) tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ ōraciyē uṭiyavi. cintuveḷiyiṉar pēciya moḻiyum ōraci uṭiyatē.
(8) teṉ intiya tirāviṭar piṇaṅkaḷit tāḻikaḷil putittaṉar; cintuveḷiyiṉarum piṇaṅkaḷit tāḻiyiṟ putittaṉar.
(9) āriyar, kiruṣṇaṉiyum avaṉ paṭi vīrariyum yamuṉik kariyil etirttaṉar; intiraṉ, kiruṣṇaṉ makaḷirik koṉṟāṉ; paṭikaḷi aḻittāṉ eṉṟu rikvētam kūṟukiṟatu. kiruṣṇaṉ, tamiḻ makkaḷatu mullinilak kaṭavuḷ: paḻamiyāṉa kaṭavuḷ.
cintuveḷiyil vāḻnta makkaḷ tirāviṭarē eṉpati uṇaralām.
āriyar vantapōtu cintuveḷiyil irunta nam muṉṉōrāṉa tirāviṭar eppaṭi iruntaṉar eṉpati, āriyar ceyta rikvētam koṇṭē aṟital nallataṉṟō?
avarkaḷ anāriyar (āriyar allātavar); taṭṭi mūkkiṉar; karu niṟattār; māṟupaṭṭa vaḻipāṭu uṭiyavar; vēṟṟu moḻiyip pēcupavar; ciṟanta celvapperukkuṭaṉ vāḻpavar; kōṭṭikaḷiyuṭiya nakaraṅkaḷil vāḻpavar; pōril ōnāy pōlap pērōci iṭupavar. avarkaḷ tācar; cuyanalakkārar; vēḷvi ceyyātavar; koṭiya moḻiyiṉar; kālnaṭikaḷi vaḷarppavar; āriyar pacukkaḷik kaḷavāṭupavar: avarkaḷil ilipiṣaṉ, tuṉi, cumuri, camparaṉ, varcciṉaṉ, tiripikaṉ, rutikraṉ, anarcaṉi, ripintaṉ, palputaṉ, pipru, vaṅktiṉ mutaliyōr aracarkaḷ āvar. ivarkaḷil vaṅritaṉ 100 nakaraṅkaḷukkut talivaṉ: camparaṉ 90 mutal 100 varippaṭṭa nakaraṅkaḷi uṭiyavaṉ. kiruṣṇaṉ 60 āyiram kaṟuppari uṭiyavaṉ; yamuṉik kariyil iruntu koṇṭu āriyari etirppavaṉ. tācar tamakkeṉṟē uriya paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷi uṭiyavar; camayak koḷkikaḷi uṭiyavar; vaṇikar; mantira māyaṅkaḷil vallavar; `kuyavaṉ, ayu eṉpavar iruvarum, taṇṇīril (taṇṇīrāl āḻappeṟṟa pātukāppu iṭattil) iruntukoṇṭu āriyarkkum, avartam kālnaṭikaṭkum tuṉpam viḷivippavar. intiraṉ 30 āyiram tācarkaḷik koṉṟāṉ; 150 paṭikaḷi aḻittāṉ. . . . . . . . 
ivaṟṟāl, nām aṟivatu `āriyar allāta tācar vaṇikarkaḷāka iruntaṉar; celvarkaḷāka iruntaṉar; pātukāppu mikunta nakaraṅkaḷil vāḻntiruntaṉar; tāca aracarkaḷ 100 nakaraṅkaḷ vari vittup pēraracu celuttiṉar; pōtiyavaṉmi peṟṟa āriyariyē etirttu niṉṟaṉar; āriyarkku viḷaṅkāta moḻiyip pēciṉar; mantira māyaṅkaḷil vallavar; tamakkoṉṟu aminta vaḻipāṭu uṭiyavar; kālnaṭikaḷi uṭiyavar; paṇattil kaṇṇāṉavar eṉpatē. ivarkaḷ āriyaruṭaṉ iṭṭa pōrkaḷiyē piṟkālap purāṇaṅkaḷil tēvarkaṭkum acurarkaṭkum naṭanta pōrkaḷāka māṟṟi eḻutiviṭṭaṉar eṉpati aṟivuṭiyōr aṟivar.
iṅṅaṉam nam muṉṉōrāṉa tirāviṭar cintu veḷiyil vāḻntapōtu maṅkōliyar, pāracīkar, alpiṉar, cumēriyar mutaliya ayal nāṭṭiṉar vāṇipattiṉ poruṭṭu aṅkuk kuṭiyēṟi iruntaṉar; muṇṭar allatu kolēriyar (intiyap paḻaṅ kuṭikaḷ) tirāviṭaruṭaṉ kalantu vāḻntaṉar. itaṉāltāṉ, cintuveḷiyil kiṭitta elumpukaḷum maṇṭi ōṭukaḷum, tirāviṭaruṭiyaṉa - maṅkōliyaruṭiyaṉa - alpiṉaruṭiyaṉa - muṇṭaruṭiyaṉavākak kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.  tōṇṭappeṟāta iṭaṅkaḷ tōṇṭap peṟiṉ, mēlum pala cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭikkum; varalāṟum uṟutippaṭum.
12. namatu kaṭami

ituvari kūṟivanta ceytikaḷāl, puti poruḷ ārāycciyē, uṇmi varalāṟu kaṇṭaṟivataṟkup peruntuṇi ceyvatu eṉṉum uṇmiyi naṉkaṟiyalām. intiyāvilēyē pukaḻpeṟṟu viḷaṅkiyavar tirāviṭarē āvar. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuḷ paḻamiyāṉatu tamiḻ oṉṟē. ataṉ ilakkaṇa ilakkiyaṅkaḷ pala. aḻintavi pōka, iṉṟu iruppavi cila. avaṟṟuḷ mikap paḻamiyāṉatu tolkāppiyam. ataṉ kuṟinta kālam ki. mu. 500 eṉa añcātu kūṟalām. eṉavē, atu iṉṟikku 2500 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ ceyyappaṭṭatu. annūl tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ pōrmuṟi, araciyal, vāḻkki nili, moḻinili mutaliyavaṟṟit tokuttuk kūṟic celkiṟatu. tolkāppiyar 1600-kku mēṟpaṭṭa cūttiraṅkaḷ ceytuḷḷār; avaṟṟil tamakku muṉ ilakkaṇap pulavar palar iruntaṉar eṉpati ēṟakkuṟiya 100 iṭaṅkaḷil cuṭṭic celkiṟār. eṉavē, 2500 āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē - tolkāppiyarkku muṉpē - tamiḻ ilakkaṇap pulavar palar iruntaṉar eṉpatu teḷivākiṟataṉṟō? at tuṇi ilakkaṇa nūlkaṭkum ilakkiya nūlkaḷ iruntiruttal vēṇṭum allavā? ilakkiyam koṇṭē ilakkaṇam ceyyappaṭutal vaḻakkam. ātalāl, tolkāppiyarkku muṟpaṭṭa ilakkaṇa āciriyar ilakkaṇa nūl ceyyap pērutavi ceytavi pala ilakkiyaṅkaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum allavā? ivaṟṟāl ēṟakkuṟiya 3000 āṇṭukaṭku (ki. mu. 1000-kku) muṉṉarē tamiḻil paṇpaṭṭa ilakkiya nūlkaḷ pala iruntaṉa eṉpati aiyamaṟa aṟiyalām.
iṉṟuḷḷa puṟanāṉūṟu eṉṉum nūlip pārttāl, vāṉmīkar pāṭiya pāṭṭum, taruma puttiraṉippaṟṟi nērē pāṭiya pāṭalum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. irāmāyaṇa - pāratakālam ki. mu, 1400-800 eṉpatu carittirāciriyar kūṟṟu. eṉavē, itikāca kālattuc (ki. mu. 1000-kku muṟpaṭṭa) ceytikaḷum tamiḻ nūlkaḷil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa: `mutal caṅkam iṉṟuḷḷa kaṉṉiyākumarikkum teṟkē irunta nāṭṭil naṭantatu; iṭic caṅkam kaṭal koṇṭa kavāṭa purattil naṭantatu eṉavarum kūṟṟukaḷ, tamiḻ nūlkaḷ ki. mu. 1500-kkum muṟpaṭṭa kālattil iruntaṉa eṉpati viḷakku kiṉṟaṉa. itu niṟka, teṉ intiyā pala āyiram āṇṭukaḷākavē nākarikam peṟṟatu eṉpataṟku aṭiyāḷamāka, eṅkut tōṇṭiṉum puti poruḷkaḷ kiṭittapaṭiyē iruk kiṉṟaṉa. avikaḷ kaṟkāla makkaḷuṭiyaṉa; cempukkāla makkaḷuṭiyaṉa; piṟkāla makkaḷuṭiyaṉa. pallāri, aṉantappūr, kaṭappi, karnūl kōṭṭaṅkaḷil kaṟkāla makkaḷiṉ maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷum 2000-kku mēṟpaṭṭa kal camātikaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa; nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa kalluḷikaḷ, vēṟu pala kaṟkaruvikaḷ, cuttikaḷ, maraccīppukaḷ, pulippaṟkaḷāl āṉa tāyittukkaḷ, kaḷimaṇ kāppukaḷ mutaliyaṉa kiṭittaṉa. pallāvarattil cintuveḷiyil kiṭittatupōlak kaḷimaṇṇum maṇalum cērttuc ceyyappeṟṟa (Tara Ctoat) maṉita uruvam kiṭittatu. pala paḻaṅkālat tāḻikaḷ kiṭittaṉa. cēlattil pallāriyil kiṭittavi pōṉṟa maṭpāṇṭaṅ kaḷum, kūntal alaṅkāram peṟṟa maṇṇālāya peṇ uruvaṅkaḷum kiṭittaṉa; kuṭicikaḷip pōṉṟa piṇap peṭṭikaḷ pala kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭaṉa.
kōyamuttūrk kōṭṭattil uḷḷa ceṭṭipāḷiyat tilum oru maṇamēṭu irukkiṟatu. ataṉuḷ kallālāṉa piṇakkōvil kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭatu. ataṟkuḷ maḻa maḻappāṉa kaṟuppuniṟa maṇpāṇṭaṅkaḷ kiṭittaṉa; mūṭiyuṭi kōppikaḷ kiṭittaṉa. kōppi mūṭimītu māṉ allatu āṭu niṟpatupōlac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ittakiya mūṭikaḷ pāracīkattil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. putukkōṭṭiyil eṇṇiṟanta kaṟkālat tāḻikaḷum, iruppuk kālattup piṇappeṭṭikaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. tiruccirāppaḷḷi, tirunelvēlik kōṭṭaṅkaḷil pala vakiyum pala niṟamum koṇṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. āticca nallūril ēṟakkuṟiya ēkkar oṉṟukku āyiram tāḻikaḷ vītam 114 ēkkarkaḷil tāḻikaḷ putikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉavām. avaṟṟuḷ civaṟṟil irumpu, veṇkalam, poṉ ivaṟṟālāṉa poruḷ kaḷum aṭakkam ceyyappeṟṟu iruntaṉa.
putuccēriyi aṭutta cempiyampāḷiyattil cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭitta viciṟippāki aṇinta peṇṇuruvam pōṉṟa oṉṟum, pala maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum maṇikaḷum kiṭittaṉa; arikkamēṭṭil kaṇakkaṟṟa nuṭpamāṉa maṇikaḷ (cintuveḷiyiṟ kiṭittaṉa pōlak) kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. putuccēriyi aṭuttuḷḷa pirañcup pakuti meyyākavē ārāyccikkuriya aṟputa iṭamākum.
āyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷākavē ciṟappup peṟṟuvanta cēra-cōḻa-pāṇṭiya talinakaraṅkaḷāṉa kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉam, vañcimā nakaram, koṟki mutaliyaṉavum, uṟiyūr, muciri, toṇṭi, kāyal mutaliyaṉavum teḷivāṉa ārāyccikku uriyavi. mohañco-tarōvip pōlavē pukār, muciri, kāyal, koṟki mutaliyaṉa ayal nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ vāṇipam ceytuvanta paṇṭi nakaraṅkaḷ. avaṟṟil iṉṟum rōma nāṇayaṅkaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. ki. mu. 1000-l vāḻnta cālamōṉ aracarkku mayilkaḷ aṉuppiya tamiḻnāṭu, kaṭal vāṇipattil ciṟantiruntatu eṉpatil aiyamuṇṭō?
ivviṭaṅkaḷi ellām cāttirīya muṟiyil ārāycci ceyyat tamiḻ makkaḷ araciyalārit tūṇṭavēṇṭum; ap paṇiyil tamiḻp paṟṟuṭiya aṟiñariyē ārāycciyāḷarāka amartta muṉital vēṇṭum; `kaṇṭa ellām āriya nākarikamē eṉac cātikka muyaluvōri appaṇiyil amarttalākātu; aṅṅaṉamē kaṇṭa ellām `tamiḻ - tirāviṭa nākarikamē eṉpavariyum amarttal ākātu. uḷḷati uḷḷavāṟu urikkum uṇmiyāḷariyē attiruppaṇi yil amarttal vēṇṭum. meyyāṉa ārāycci naṭipeṟiṉ, intiya carittira ulakam viyappuṟumāṟu ariya poruḷkaḷ kiṭikkum; tirāviṭar toṉmi nākarikam ulakeṅkum uṇara vaḻi uṇṭākum: viṉceṉṭ mit kūṟiṉāṟpōla, intiya carittiram vikiyāṟṟiliruntu kaṭṭappeṟum annāḷē uṇmi varalāṟu veḷivanta naṉṉāḷ ākum.
